###############################################################################
# Vampire events
# Namespace: vamp (was "svampires" before the 2026 events refactor; the
# necromancy ritual events in wh_magic_system_power.txt keep "svampires")
# Faction: Vampires
#
# The vampire bloodlines of the mod: sires and their get, the Carstein Ring,
# the Blood Dragons, the Strigoi and the dream of Strygos, the Strigany
# caravans, the Necrarchs' experiments, the Jade vampires and their Dark
# Crystals, the Mahtmasi of the Shadowrealm and their voodoo dolls, the
# Blood Quest of the Knights of Abhorash (was "sblooddragons" in
# S_vampire_questing_events.txt) and the intrigues of the Lahmian Sisterhood
# (was "lahmia" in BR_lahmian_events.txt), the dark gift, covens and sires
# (was "vampire" in BR_vampire_system.txt), and Vlad von Carstein's Vampire
# Wars (was "carstein" in BR_Von_Carstein_events.txt and "vladsub" in
# I_Vlads_Subjugation.txt).
#
# Sires and their get                   vamp.1 - vamp.19
# The Carstein Ring                     vamp.20 - vamp.42
# Blood Dragons                         vamp.50 - vamp.58
# Strigoi and Strygos                   vamp.60 - vamp.77
# Strigany caravans                     vamp.80 - vamp.98
# Necrarchs                             vamp.100 - vamp.117
# Jade vampires                         vamp.120 - vamp.166
# Mahtmasi and the Shadowrealm          vamp.170 - vamp.189
# The voodoo doll plot                  vamp.200 - vamp.271
# The Blood Quest                       vamp.300 - vamp.352
# The Lahmian Sisterhood                vamp.400 - vamp.474
# The dark gift and the coven           vamp.500 - vamp.516
# Sire and get                          vamp.520 - vamp.528
# The Vampire Wars                      vamp.600 - vamp.631
#
# Fired from common/on_actions/wh_on_actions.txt: vamp.60 every year;
# vamp.120, 77 and 148 every five years; the bi-yearly list vamp_sire_demands,
# the five-year lists vamp_blood_tribute, vamp_blood_bride, vamp_ghouls,
# vamp_jealousy, vamp_sire_rivalry, vamp_mahtmasi_love and
# vamp_mahtmasi_hunger, and the decade list (vamp.160); on_startup (vamp.50),
# on_death (vamp.1, 97, 98), on_siege_over_winner (vamp.100, 155),
# on_new_holder (vamp.180) and on_war_ended_defeat (vamp.182); vamp.470
# every year, vamp.450 every two years, vamp.400 on_startup, vamp.306 on_death
# and vamp.306/307 on_become_imprisoned_any_reason; vamp.600 on_startup,
# vamp.605 every year and vamp.609 on_death. The vlad_subjugation CB in
# common/cb_types/wh_cb_types.txt fires Vlad's offer (vamp.630), the
# refugees (vamp.620, 621) and
# antideath_effect the Carstein Ring revival (vamp.615).
# Decisions in wh_vampire_decisions.txt, wh_orient_decisions.txt,
# wh_magic_decisions.txt and wh_general_decisions.txt start most chains;
# the Mahtmasi kiss (vamp.175 - 178) comes from wh_scripted_effects.txt.
# The two plots are plot_steal_carstein_ring and plot_make_voodoo_doll.
# Scripted effects: common/scripted_effects/wh_vamp_effects.txt
# Scripted triggers: common/scripted_triggers/wh_vamp_triggers.txt
# Event modifiers: common/event_modifiers/wh_vampire_modifiers.txt
# Artifacts: common/artifacts/24_artifacts_vamp.txt
# Loc: localisation/00_WHG_vampire_events.csv
# Full list with callers: docs/events/event_database.csv
###############################################################################

namespace = vamp

###############################################################################
# Sires and their get
###############################################################################

# vamp.1 A sire's death
# When a vampire ruler dies, every vampire in the realm outside the dynasty
# (the thralls they made) gets a claim on the dead ruler's primary title
# (vamp.2).
# Scope: ROOT is the dead ruler.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.1
	hide_window = yes

	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_death

	trigger = {
		is_vampire_visible_trigger = yes
		any_realm_character = { is_vampire_visible_trigger = yes NOT = { dynasty = ROOT } }
	}
	immediate = {
		any_realm_character = {
			limit = { is_vampire_visible_trigger = yes NOT = { dynasty = ROOT } }
			character_event = { id = vamp.2 }
		}
	}
}

# vamp.2 Claim on the sire's title
# Hidden. Gives the thrall a claim on FROM's primary title.
# Scope: ROOT is the thrall, FROM the dead sire.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.2
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = { primary_title = { ROOT = { add_claim = PREV } } }
	}
}

# vamp.3 A thrall asks for a duchy
# A landless or baron vampire made by a liege of their dynasty who holds two
# or more duchies asks the sire for one of them (vamp.4).
# Scope: ROOT is the thrall.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.3
	desc = EVTDESCvamp3
	picture = "GFX_evt_vampire_politics"

	min_age = 16

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_bi_yearly_pulse, random list vamp_sire_demands

	trigger = {
		OR = { has_landed_title = no lower_tier_than = COUNT }
		is_vampire_visible_trigger = yes
		any_dynasty_member = {
			ROOT = { has_opinion_modifier = { modifier = opinion_sired_me who = PREV } }
			ROOT = { liege = { character = PREVPREV } }
			demesne_size = 2
			num_of_duke_titles = 2
			higher_tier_than = DUKE
		}
		NOT = { trait = incapable }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1
		modifier = { factor = 3 trait = ambitious }
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = proud }
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = greedy }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = content }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = humble }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp3
		random_dynasty_member = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = { has_opinion_modifier = { modifier = opinion_sired_me who = PREV } }
				ROOT = { liege = { character = PREVPREV } }
				demesne_size = 2
				num_of_duke_titles = 2
				higher_tier_than = DUKE
			}
			character_event = { id = vamp.4 days = 1 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIP66051 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBvamp3
		trigger = { ai = no }
	}
}

# vamp.4 The thrall's request for a duchy
# The sire grants a duchy that is not their primary title (vamp.5) or refuses
# (vamp.6) and earns the thrall's lasting resentment.
# Scope: ROOT is the sire, FROM the thrall.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.4
	desc = EVTDESCvamp4
	picture = "GFX_evt_vampire_politics"

	is_triggered_only = yes

	#no need for notifactions back, landless character gets it anyway.
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp4

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = { factor = 2 from = { diplomacy = 14 } }
			modifier = { factor = 2 from = { martial = 14 } }
			modifier = { factor = 2 from = { intrigue = 14 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 opinion = { who = from value = 25 } }
			modifier = { factor = 2 opinion = { who = from value = 50 } }
			modifier = { factor = 3 opinion = { who = from value = 75 } }
		}
		prestige = -5
		FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.5 } }
		random_demesne_title = {
			limit = { tier = DUKE NOT = { is_primary_holder_title = yes } }
			grant_title = FROM
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBvamp4
		FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.6 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 2 modifier = { factor = 0 opinion = { who = from value = 80 } } }

		FROM = { opinion = { modifier = dynastic_refused_title who = ROOT years = 100 } }
	}
}

# vamp.7 A thrall asks for a county
# Like vamp.3, for an unlanded thrall whose sire holds four or more counties.
# Scope: ROOT is the thrall.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.7
	desc = EVTDESCvamp3
	picture = "GFX_evt_vampire_politics"

	min_age = 16

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_bi_yearly_pulse, random list vamp_sire_demands

	trigger = {
		is_ruler = no
		is_vampire_visible_trigger = yes
		any_dynasty_member = {
			ROOT = { has_opinion_modifier = { modifier = opinion_sired_me who = PREV } }
			ROOT = { liege = { character = PREVPREV } }
			demesne_size = 4
			num_of_count_titles = 4
			higher_tier_than = COUNT
		}
		NOT = { trait = incapable }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1
		modifier = { factor = 3 trait = ambitious }
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = proud }
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = greedy }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = content }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = humble }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp3
		random_dynasty_member = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = { has_opinion_modifier = { modifier = opinion_sired_me who = PREV } }
				ROOT = { liege = { character = PREVPREV } }
				demesne_size = 4
				num_of_count_titles = 4
				higher_tier_than = COUNT
			}
			character_event = { id = vamp.8 days = 1 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIP66051 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBvamp3
		trigger = { ai = no }
	}
}

# vamp.10 The blood kiss
# The turning decision used on someone under 16: the child is told who turned
# them and the sire is notified (vamp.11).
# Scope: ROOT is the child, FROMFROM the vampire.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.10
	title = UNDEATH
	picture = "GFX_evt_vampire_kiss"
	desc = EVTDESCvamp10

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp10
		opinion = { modifier = opinion_sired_me who = FROMFROM months = 240000 }
		FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.11 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
}

# vamp.11 A new child of the night
# The child joins the ranks of the undead.
# Scope: ROOT is the sire, FROM the new vampire.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.11
	title = UNDEATH
	desc = EVTDESCvamp11
	picture = "GFX_evt_vampire_kiss"

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp11
		root_makes_from_vampire_effect = yes
		recalc_succession = yes
	}
}

# vamp.15 Jealous lover
# A vampire whose vampire lover keeps other lovers too demands to be the only
# one (vamp.16). The kind and charitable may choose to share.
# Scope: ROOT is the jealous vampire.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.15
	desc = EVTDESCvamp15
	picture = GFX_evt_vampire_politics
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_five_year_pulse, random list vamp_jealousy

	religion_group = necromantic_group
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no

	trigger = {
		is_vampire_trigger = yes
		cant_love_trigger = yes
		NOT = { trait = lahmian_queen }
		any_lover = { is_vampire_trigger = yes num_of_lovers = 2 cant_love_trigger = yes }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = wroth }
		modifier = { factor = 1.7 trait = vampire_lahmian_visible }
		modifier = { factor = 1.4 trait = vampire_carstein_visible }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = vampire_jade_visible }
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = envious }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = cruel }
		modifier = { factor = 0.8 trait = charitable }
		modifier = { factor = 0.8 trait = kind }
		modifier = { factor = 0.8 trait = patient }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp15
		random_lover = {
			limit = { is_vampire_trigger = yes num_of_lovers = 2 cant_love_trigger = yes }
			character_event = { id = vamp.16 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBvamp15
		trigger = { OR = { trait = charitable trait = kind } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

# vamp.16 An ultimatum
# The unfaithful lover either sends the other lovers away (vamp.18, vamp.19)
# or leaves the jealous one (vamp.17).
# Scope: ROOT is the unfaithful lover, FROM the jealous vampire.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.16
	desc = EVTDESCvamp16
	picture = GFX_evt_vampire_politics

	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no

	trigger = {
		is_vampire_trigger = yes
		cant_love_trigger = yes
		has_lover = yes
		any_lover = { cant_love_trigger = yes is_vampire_trigger = yes num_of_lovers = 2 }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp16
		any_lover = {
			limit = { NOT = { character = FROM } cant_love_trigger = yes }
			character_event = { id = vamp.19 }
		}
		any_lover = { limit = { character = FROM } character_event = { id = vamp.18 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBvamp16
		any_lover = { limit = { character = FROM } character_event = { id = vamp.17 } }
	}
}

# vamp.17 Spurned
# The jealous vampire was dropped and becomes the unfaithful lover's rival.
# Scope: ROOT is the jealous vampire, FROM the unfaithful lover.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.17
	desc = EVTDESCvamp17
	picture = GFX_evt_vampire_politics

	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp17
		remove_lover = FROM
		add_rival = FROM
	}
}

###############################################################################
# The Carstein Ring
###############################################################################

# vamp.20 The ring is taken
# Hidden. Passes the Carstein Ring decision on to whoever holds the ring's
# thief (vamp.21).
# Scope: ROOT is the decision taker.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.20
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.21 days = 5 } }
	}
}

# vamp.21 What to do with the ring?
# The holder of a stolen Carstein Ring may keep it (vampires only; gains
# claims on the old owner's titles if necromantic) or destroy it. Players
# hear about it (vamp.22 or vamp.612).
# Scope: ROOT is the new holder, FROM the ring's old owner.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.21
	desc = EVTDESCvamp21
	picture = "GFX_evt_carstein"
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	trigger = {
		OR = { has_character_flag = prisoner_claim_ring has_plot = plot_steal_carstein_ring }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp21
		#Steal for yourself
		trigger = { is_vampire_trigger = yes }
		FROM = { destroy_artifact = carstein_ring }
		add_artifact = carstein_ring
		if = {
			limit = { religion_group = necromantic_group }
			piety = 250
			prestige = 250
			FROM = { any_demesne_title = { ROOT = { add_claim = PREV } } }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			any_playable_ruler = {
				limit = { ai = no vamp_old_world_culture_trigger = yes }
				character_event = { id = vamp.22 days = 7 }
			}
			any_playable_ruler = {
				limit = { ai = no vamp_old_world_culture_trigger = no }
				character_event = { id = vamp.22 days = 30 }
			}
		}
		clr_character_flag = prisoner_claim_ring
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBvamp21
		#Destroy it
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 modifier = { factor = 0 is_vampire_trigger = yes } }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			FROM = { destroy_artifact = carstein_ring }
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { religion_group = necromantic_group } }
				piety = 1000
				prestige = 500
			}
			any_playable_ruler = {
				limit = { ai = no vamp_old_world_culture_trigger = yes }
				character_event = { id = vamp.612 days = 7 }
			}
			any_playable_ruler = {
				limit = { ai = no vamp_old_world_culture_trigger = no }
				character_event = { id = vamp.612 days = 30 }
			}
			clr_character_flag = prisoner_claim_ring
		}
	}
}

# vamp.22 The ring has a new master
# Another vampire has taken the Carstein Ring. Notification for players.
# Scope: ROOT is the player, FROM the new holder.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.22
	desc = EVTDESCvamp22
	picture = "GFX_evt_carstein"
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp22
	}
}

# vamp.25 The ring thief: the plotter's chance
# The head of a plot to steal the Carstein Ring sees an opening and hires a
# thief, paid by the plot backers, from their own purse or with their own
# cunning (learning). The thief strikes a month later (vamp.30).
# Scope: ROOT is the plotter.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.25
	desc = EVTDESCvamp25
	picture = GFX_evt_whispers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	is_part_of_plot = yes

	trigger = {
		has_plot = plot_steal_carstein_ring
		plot_target_char = { age = 16 prisoner = no NOT = { trait = incapable } }
		NOT = { has_character_flag = ringstealing_in_motion }
		plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.025 }
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		years = 700

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.7
			plot_target_char = { check_variable = { which = carstein_death_count value = 3 } }
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			plot_target_char = { check_variable = { which = carstein_death_count value = 5 } }
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			plot_target_char = { check_variable = { which = carstein_death_count value = 8 } }
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			plot_target_char = { check_variable = { which = carstein_death_count value = 10 } }
		}
		modifier = { factor = 0.6 plot_target_char = { in_command = yes } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.6 plot_target_char = { realm_size = 150 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.8 learning = 10 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.8 learning = 15 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.8 learning = 20 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.8 learning = 25 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 has_focus = focus_intrigue }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 trait = schemer }
		modifier = { factor = 1.7 plot_target_char = { trait = in_hiding } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.40 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.50 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.60 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.70 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.80 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.90 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 1.0 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 1.5 } }

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = { has_plot = plot_steal_carstein_ring plot_power = 1.0 }
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = { has_plot = plot_steal_carstein_ring plot_power = 1.5 }
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = { has_plot = plot_steal_carstein_ring plot_power = 2.0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp25 # The plot pays
		trigger = { plot_power = 1.0 }
		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = vamp.37 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = ringstealing_in_motion
		plot_target_char = {
			character_event = { id = vamp.30 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPvamp30 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBvamp25 # Pay from my own purse
		trigger = { NOT = { plot_power = 0.5 } NOT = { learning = 10 } }
		scaled_wealth = -1.0

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = vamp.37 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = ringstealing_in_motion
		plot_target_char = {
			character_event = { id = vamp.30 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPvamp30 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBvamp25 # Pay from my own purse
		trigger = { plot_power = 0.5 NOT = { plot_power = 1.0 } NOT = { learning = 10 } }
		scaled_wealth = -0.5

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = vamp.37 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = ringstealing_in_motion
		plot_target_char = {
			character_event = { id = vamp.30 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPvamp30 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCvamp25 # My own cunning
		tooltip_info = learning
		trigger = { plot_power = 0.5 learning = 10 }

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = vamp.37 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = ringstealing_in_motion
		plot_target_char = {
			character_event = { id = vamp.30 days = 30 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPvamp30 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDvamp25 # I cannot afford this
		trigger = { ai = no NOT = { learning = 10 } NOT = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
	}
}

# vamp.26 The ring thief: a backer's chance
# A backer of the ring plot finds a thief and tells the plotter (vamp.35) or
# asks the plotter for funds (vamp.27).
# Scope: ROOT is the backer.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.26
	desc = EVTDESCvamp26
	picture = GFX_evt_whispers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	is_part_of_plot = yes

	trigger = {
		any_backed_character = {
			has_plot = plot_steal_carstein_ring
			plot_power = 0.5
			plot_target_char = { age = 16 prisoner = no NOT = { trait = incapable } }
			NOT = { has_character_flag = ringstealing_in_motion }
			ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.025 } }
		}
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		years = 350

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.7
			plot_target_char = { check_variable = { which = carstein_death_count value = 3 } }
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			plot_target_char = { check_variable = { which = carstein_death_count value = 5 } }
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			plot_target_char = { check_variable = { which = carstein_death_count value = 8 } }
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			plot_target_char = { check_variable = { which = carstein_death_count value = 10 } }
		}
		modifier = { factor = 0.6 plot_target_char = { in_command = yes } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.6 plot_target_char = { realm_size = 150 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.8 learning = 10 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.8 learning = 15 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.8 learning = 20 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.8 learning = 25 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 has_focus = focus_intrigue }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 trait = schemer }
		modifier = { factor = 1.7 plot_target_char = { trait = in_hiding } }
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				has_plot = plot_steal_carstein_ring
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.10 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				has_plot = plot_steal_carstein_ring
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.20 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				has_plot = plot_steal_carstein_ring
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.30 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				has_plot = plot_steal_carstein_ring
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.40 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				has_plot = plot_steal_carstein_ring
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.50 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				has_plot = plot_steal_carstein_ring
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.60 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				has_plot = plot_steal_carstein_ring
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.70 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				has_plot = plot_steal_carstein_ring
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.80 } }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				has_plot = plot_steal_carstein_ring
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.90 } }
			}
		}

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = { has_plot = plot_steal_carstein_ring plot_power = 1.0 }
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = { has_plot = plot_steal_carstein_ring plot_power = 1.5 }
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = { has_plot = plot_steal_carstein_ring plot_power = 2.0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp26 # The plot pays
		trigger = {
			any_backed_character = { has_plot = plot_steal_carstein_ring plot_power = 1.0 }
		}
		random_backed_character = {
			limit = { has_plot = plot_steal_carstein_ring plot_power = 1.0 }
			character_event = { id = vamp.35 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPvamp35 }
			set_character_flag = ringstealing_in_motion
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBvamp26 # Ask the Plotter for funds
		trigger = {
			NOT = { learning = 10 }
			NOT = {
				any_backed_character = { has_plot = plot_steal_carstein_ring plot_power = 1.0 }
			}
		}
		random_backed_character = {
			limit = { has_plot = plot_steal_carstein_ring NOT = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
			character_event = { id = vamp.27 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPvamp27 }
			set_character_flag = ringstealing_in_motion
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTEvamp26 # Pay from my own purse
		trigger = {
			NOT = { learning = 10 }
			NOT = {
				any_backed_character = { has_plot = plot_steal_carstein_ring plot_power = 1.0 }
			}
		}
		scaled_wealth = -0.5
		random_backed_character = {
			limit = { has_plot = plot_steal_carstein_ring NOT = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
			character_event = { id = vamp.35 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPvamp35 }
			set_character_flag = ringstealing_in_motion
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCvamp26 # My own cunning
		tooltip_info = learning
		trigger = {
			learning = 10
			any_backed_character = { has_plot = plot_steal_carstein_ring plot_power = 0.5 }
		}

		random_backed_character = {
			limit = { has_plot = plot_steal_carstein_ring plot_power = 0.5 }
			character_event = { id = vamp.35 days = 3 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPvamp35 }
			set_character_flag = ringstealing_in_motion
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDvamp26 # I cannot afford this
		trigger = {
			ai = no
			NOT = { learning = 10 }
			NOT = {
				any_backed_character = { has_plot = plot_steal_carstein_ring plot_power = 1.0 }
			}
		}
	}
}

		name = EVTOPTBvamp27

# vamp.30 A touch in the death-sleep
# The thief comes for the ring while the target rests. The paranoid, the
# lazy and commanders are harder to rob. Theft and discovery are rolled
# separately (vamp.31 to vamp.34, plotter notes vamp.38 to vamp.41).
# Scope: ROOT is the ring holder, FROM the plotter or backer, FROMFROM the
# plotter.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.30
	desc = EVTDESCvamp30
	picture = GFX_evt_shadow
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		prisoner = no

		# Is the plot still in motion?
		OR = {
			FROM = { has_plot = plot_steal_carstein_ring plot_target_char = { character = ROOT } }
			FROMFROM = {
				has_plot = plot_steal_carstein_ring
				plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		save_event_target_as = target_victim
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp30 # Sleeps on
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					AND = { trait = paranoid random = 33 }
					AND = { trait = slothful random = 50 }
					AND = { in_command = yes random = 90 }
					AND = {
						NOT = { trait = slothful }
						NOT = { in_command = yes }
						NOT = { trait = paranoid }
						random = 75
					}
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				25 = {
					FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.38 days = 1 } }
					FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.38 days = 1 } }
					character_event = { id = vamp.31 days = 1 } # Stolen, thief discovered
				}
				75 = {
					FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.39 days = 1 } }
					FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.39 days = 1 } }
					character_event = { id = vamp.32 days = 1 } # Stolen, thief unknown
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBvamp30 # Wakes in time
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = { trait = paranoid random = 33 }
				AND = { trait = slothful random = 50 }
				AND = { in_command = yes random = 90 }
				AND = {
					NOT = { trait = slothful }
					NOT = { in_command = yes }
					NOT = { trait = paranoid }
					random = 75
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.40 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.40 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = vamp.33 days = 1 } # Not stolen, thief discovered
				}
				50 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.41 days = 1 } }
						FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.41 days = 1 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = vamp.34 days = 1 } # Not stolen, thief unknown
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# vamp.31 Robbed, and the thief is known
# The ring is gone and the target knows who sent the thief, who may vanish
# with it (vamp.42) or keep it (vamp.21).
# Scope: ROOT is the robbed ring holder.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.31
	desc = EVTDESCvamp31
	picture = GFX_evt_shadow
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp31
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					FROMFROMFROM = {
						has_plot = plot_steal_carstein_ring
						plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
					}
				}
				opinion = { who = FROMFROMFROM modifier = opinion_stolen_ring months = 24000 }
				FROMFROMFROM = {
					random_list = {
						70 = { character_event = { id = vamp.42 days = 5 random = 7 } } #the thief disappears
						30 = { character_event = { id = vamp.21 days = 5 random = 7 } }
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_alive = yes }
				opinion = { who = FROMFROM modifier = opinion_stolen_ring months = 24000 }
				FROMFROM = {
					random_list = {
						70 = { character_event = { id = vamp.42 days = 5 random = 7 } } #the thief disappears
						30 = { character_event = { id = vamp.21 days = 5 random = 7 } }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# vamp.40 Failure, and exposed
# The thief was caught and named the plotter; the plot is revealed.
# Scope: ROOT is a plotter, FROM the ring holder.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.40
	desc = EVTDESCvamp40
	picture = GFX_evt_shadowy_cabal
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		character_event = { id = 11 } # Consequences of discovery
		clear_event_target = target_victim

		if = {
			limit = { plot_target_char = { character = FROM } has_plot = plot_steal_carstein_ring }
			reveal_plot = yes
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp40
		if = {
			limit = { plot_target_char = { character = FROM } has_plot = plot_steal_carstein_ring }
			clr_character_flag = ringstealing_in_motion
			tooltip = { reveal_plot = yes }
		}
	}
}

# vamp.50 The Red Duke rises
# At game start the Red Duke of Aquitaine rises: retinues of Black Knights,
# Grave Guard and Blood Knights, and for the AI money and a Blood Knight host
# to start the war at once.
# Scope: ROOT is the holder of k_red_dukedom.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.50
	desc = EVTDESCvamp50
	picture = GFX_evt_vampire_knight
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_startup
	trigger = {
		has_landed_title = k_red_dukedom
		NOT = { has_character_flag = started_duke }
		trait = vampire_blood_visible
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp50
		set_character_flag = started_duke
		hidden_tooltip = {
			create_retinue = RETTYPE_V_CRYPTHORRORS
			create_retinue = RETTYPE_V_BLACKKNIGHTS
			create_retinue = RETTYPE_V_BLACKKNIGHTS
			create_retinue = RETTYPE_V_BLACKKNIGHTS
			create_retinue = RETTYPE_V_GRAVEGUARD
			create_retinue = RETTYPE_UNDEAD_HORDE
			create_retinue = RETTYPE_UNDEAD_HORDE
			create_retinue = RETTYPE_UNDEAD_HORDE
			create_retinue = RETTYPE_UNDEAD_HORDE
			create_retinue = RETTYPE_V_VAMPIREKNIGHTS
		}
		location = {
			ROOT = {
				if = {
					limit = { ai = yes }
					wealth = 250
					piety = 250
					spawn_unit = {
						province = PREV
						reinforces = yes
						troops = { vampire_knights = { 250 250 } }
					}
					spawn_unit = {
						province = PREV
						reinforces = yes
						troops = { knights = { 300 300 } }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBvamp50
		set_character_flag = started_duke
		if = { limit = { ai = yes } wealth = 250 piety = 250 }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			create_retinue = RETTYPE_V_CAIRNWRAITHS
			create_retinue = RETTYPE_V_CAIRNWRAITHS
			create_retinue = RETTYPE_V_GRAVEGUARD
			create_retinue = RETTYPE_V_GRAVEGUARD
			create_retinue = RETTYPE_UNDEAD_HORDE
			create_retinue = RETTYPE_UNDEAD_HORDE
			create_retinue = RETTYPE_V_VAMPIREKNIGHTS
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

# vamp.52 Spoils for the Grand Master
# A Blood Dragon mercenary captain sends trinkets from the quest to the master
# of the order (vamp.53). About once in 30 years per captain.
# Scope: ROOT is the captain.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.52
	desc = EVTDESCvamp52
	picture = GFX_evt_vampire_knight
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_five_year_pulse, random list vamp_blood_tribute

	capable_only = yes
	religion_group = necromantic_group

	trigger = {
		trait = vampire_blood_visible
		prestige = 25
		piety = 25
		primary_title = { mercenary = yes adventurer = yes }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1
		modifier = { factor = 1.2 trait = ambitious }
		modifier = { factor = 1.2 trait = brave }
		modifier = { factor = 0.55 trait = craven }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = content }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp52
		prestige = 200
		wealth = 100
		piety = 100
		if = {
			limit = { band_creator = { is_alive = yes } }
			band_creator = { character_event = { id = vamp.53 } }
		}
	}
}

# vamp.53 Tribute from the quest
# The master of the order receives the spoils, sometimes the blade of a fallen
# champion (artifact vamp_quest_trophy).
# Scope: ROOT is the band's founder, FROM the captain.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.53
	desc = EVTDESCvamp53
	picture = GFX_evt_vampire_knight
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	capable_only = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp53
		prestige = 100
		wealth = 250
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_artifact = vamp_quest_trophy } }
			random = { chance = 33 add_artifact = vamp_quest_trophy }
		}
	}
}

# vamp.54 A worthy recruit
# A Blood Dragon mercenary captain picks a strong young courtier, or a child,
# of the employer's court, turns them and takes them away. The employer
# (vamp.55) and the band's founder (vamp.56) are told. About once in 30 years
# per captain.
# Scope: ROOT is the captain.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.54
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_five_year_pulse, random list vamp_blood_bride

	trigger = {
		primary_title = { mercenary = yes band_creator = { is_alive = yes } }
		trait = vampire_blood_visible
		NOT = { trait = chaste }
		NOT = { trait = celibate }

		primary_title = {
			capital_scope = {
				owner = {
					any_courtier = {
						is_landed = no
						is_married = no
						is_consort = no
						is_adult = yes
						NOT = { trait = incapable }
						NOT = { trait = infirm }
						NOT = { trait = maimed }
						NOT = { trait = blinded }
						NOT = { trait = eunuch }
						NOT = { trait = is_malnourished }
						NOT = { trait = is_fat }
						NOT = { age = 40 }
						is_vampire_trigger = no
						trait = creature_human
						OR = {
							is_child_of = PREV
							martial = 20
							trait = strong
							trait = tough
							trait = hardy
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1

		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = cruel }

		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = greedy }

		modifier = { factor = 2 has_character_modifier = bloodthirsty }

		modifier = { factor = 1.5 mercenary_employer = { is_alive = yes } }

		modifier = {
			factor = 1.25
			primary_title = { capital_scope = { owner = { any_liege = { is_friend = ROOT } } } }
		}

		modifier = { factor = 1.25 mercenary_employer = { is_friend = ROOT } }
	}

	immediate = {
		primary_title = {
			capital_scope = {
				owner = {
					random_courtier = {
						limit = {
							is_landed = no
							is_married = no
							is_consort = no
							is_adult = yes
							NOT = { trait = incapable }
							NOT = { trait = infirm }
							NOT = { trait = maimed }
							NOT = { trait = blinded }
							NOT = { trait = eunuch }
							NOT = { trait = is_malnourished }
							NOT = { trait = is_fat }
							NOT = { age = 40 }
							is_vampire_trigger = no
							trait = creature_human
							OR = {
								is_child_of = PREV
								martial = 20
								trait = strong
								trait = tough
								trait = hardy
							}
						}

						save_event_target_as = eloper
						PREV = { save_event_target_as = eloper_host }

						ROOT = {
							primary_title = {
								band_creator = {
									if = {
										limit = { is_alive = yes }
										character_event = { id = vamp.56 }
									}
								}
							}
						}

						PREV = { character_event = { id = vamp.55 } }

						any_spouse = { remove_spouse = ROOT }
						any_lover = { remove_lover = ROOT }
						any_friend = { remove_friend = ROOT }
						clear_favour_effect = yes
						clr_character_flag = hidden_cultist_tzeentch
						clr_character_flag = hidden_cultist_nurgle
						clr_character_flag = hidden_cultist_slaanesh
						clr_character_flag = hidden_cultist_khorne
						clr_character_flag = hidden_cultist_undivided
						add_trait = vampire_blood_visible
						set_graphical_culture = blood_vampire
						culture = blooddragons_culture
						religion = vampiric
						vamp_join_bloodline_effect = yes
						remove_trait = old_1
						remove_trait = old_2
						remove_trait = old_3
						remove_trait = old_4
						move_character = ROOT
						if = { limit = { is_child_of = PREV } PREV = { add_rival = ROOT } }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# vamp.58 A Blood Dragon fortress
# If the occupation held, a castle is built in the province for the Blood
# Dragon.
# Scope: ROOT is the province, FROM the title, FROMFROM the Blood Dragon.
province_event = {
	id = vamp.58
	desc = EVTDESCvamp58
	picture = GFX_evt_vampire_knight
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		OR = { FROM = { is_occupied = yes } FROM = { holder_scope = { character = FROMFROM } } }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp58
		build_holding = { type = castle holder = FROMFROM }
	}
}

# vamp.60 Waking from hibernation
# Hidden. Clears hidden_hibernated_strigoi from the capital of a ruler who is
# no longer resting (woken by any event or decision).
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.60
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_pulse

	only_rulers = yes

	trigger = {
		capital_scope = { has_province_modifier = hidden_hibernated_strigoi }
		NOT = { trait = resting }
	}

	immediate = {
		capital_scope = { remove_province_modifier = hidden_hibernated_strigoi }
	}
}

# vamp.61 Ghouls gather
# Ghouls and lesser undead flock to an old vampire ruler. Costs piety; the
# ghouls join the army and settle in the capital (ghoul_presence). Strigoi
# draw the most, Lahmians and Blood Dragons the fewest.
# Scope: ROOT is the vampire ruler.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.61
	desc = EVTDESCvamp61
	picture = GFX_evt_ghoul
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_five_year_pulse, random list vamp_ghouls

	min_age = 250 # Only old vampires
	only_rulers = yes
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	religion_group = necromantic_group

	trigger = {
		higher_tier_than = BARON
		is_incapacitated_trigger = no
		is_vampire_visible_trigger = yes
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1
		modifier = { factor = 1.33 trait = vampire_strigoi_visible }
		modifier = { factor = 0.36 trait = vampire_blood_visible }
		modifier = { factor = 0.67 trait = vampire_carstein_visible }
		modifier = { factor = 0.1 trait = vampire_lahmian_visible }
		modifier = { factor = 0.33 independent = no }
		modifier = { factor = 0.14 independent = no lower_tier_than = KING }
		modifier = { factor = 0.67 NOT = { piety = 1500 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.67 NOT = { piety = 1000 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.67 NOT = { piety = 750 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.67 NOT = { piety = 500 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.67 NOT = { piety = 250 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.67 NOT = { piety = 100 } }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp61
		piety = -250
		hidden_tooltip = {
			location = {
				ROOT = {
					if = {
						limit = { trait = vampire_strigoi_visible }
						spawn_unit = {
							province = PREV
							reinforces = yes
							troops = { light_infantry = { 1176 1176 } }
							earmark = shambling_ghouls
							attrition = 0.75
						}
						spawn_unit = {
							province = PREV
							reinforces = yes
							troops = { undead_horde = { 781 781 } }
							earmark = shambling_ghouls
							attrition = 0
						}
					}
					else = {
						spawn_unit = {
							province = PREV
							reinforces = yes
							troops = { light_infantry = { 576 576 } }
							earmark = shambling_ghouls
							attrition = 0.75
						}
						spawn_unit = {
							province = PREV
							reinforces = yes
							troops = { undead_horde = { 911 911 } }
							earmark = shambling_ghouls
							attrition = 0
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { capital_scope = { has_province_modifier = ghoul_presence } }
			random_demesne_province = {
				add_province_modifier = { name = ghoul_presence duration = 1750 stacking = yes }
			}
		}
		else = {
			capital_scope = {
				add_province_modifier = { name = ghoul_presence duration = 1750 stacking = yes }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 75
			modifier = { factor = 0 trait = vampire_blood_visible }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = vampire_carstein_visible }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 independent = no lower_tier_than = KING }
			modifier = { factor = 1.75 war = yes }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBvamp61
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 25
			modifier = { factor = 3 trait = vampire_lahmian_visible }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = vampire_jade_visible }
			modifier = { factor = 100 trait = vampire_blood_visible }
			modifier = { factor = 0 trait = vampire_strigoi_visible }
		}
	}
}

# vamp.62 The muster goes on
# Hidden. While the Strigoi invasion is prepared, roll for the next arrival
# each month (vamp_strigoi_muster_effect).
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler preparing the invasion.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.62
	hide_window = yes

	trigger = {
		has_character_modifier = preparing_strygos_invasion
	} #the chain stops when the modifier expires
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		vamp_strigoi_muster_effect = yes
	}
}

# vamp.63 You waited too long
# The invasion was not launched in time: the gathered host leaves.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.63
	desc = { text = EVTDESCvamp63 trigger = { NOT = { trait = vampire_strigoi_visible } } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCvamp63b trigger = { trait = vampire_strigoi_visible } }
	picture = GFX_evt_vampires_orcs
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	trigger = {
		NOT = { any_war = { using_cb = strigoi_invasion } }
	} #If you start the war, the event won't pop up

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp63
		disband_event_forces = prepared_invasion
		remove_character_modifier = preparing_strygos_invasion
		prestige = -1000
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = attempted_strygos_invasion duration = 3651 }
	}
}

# vamp.67 The AI attacks
# Hidden. An AI that prepared the invasion declares the war on an independent
# ruler holding the old lands of Strygos.
# Scope: ROOT is the AI ruler.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.67
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		random_independent_ruler = {
			limit = { primary_title = { capital_scope = { region = world_badlands_strygos } } }
			primary_title = {
				holder_scope = {
					ROOT = {
						war = {
							casus_belli = strigoi_invasion
							target = PREV # The top liege of the owner of the province
							thirdparty_title = PREVPREV # The de jure kingdom
							tier = KING
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# vamp.70 Strygos restored by a Strigoi
# A Strigoi restores the empire of Strygos (e_strygos) and Mourkain becomes
# the capital. The Lahmian queen (vamp.72) and the other Strigoi (vamp.73)
# hear of it; the new emperor may revive the old ways later (vamp.74).
# Scope: ROOT is the new emperor.
long_character_event = {
	id = vamp.70
	desc = EVTDESCvamp70
	picture = GFX_evt_strigoi_lord

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp70
		set_global_flag = strygos_reformed

		hidden_tooltip = {
			character_event = { id = vamp.74 days = 700 random = 2000 }
			primary_title = {
				any_direct_de_jure_vassal_title = { de_jure_liege = e_strygos }
				k_khernarch = { de_jure_liege = e_strygos }
				k_strygos = { de_jure_liege = e_strygos }
				k_badlands = { de_jure_liege = e_strygos }
			}
			any_playable_ruler = {
				limit = { trait = lahmian_queen }
				character_event = { id = vamp.72 days = 5 random = 4 }
			}
			any_playable_ruler = {
				limit = { trait = vampire_strigoi_visible }
				character_event = { id = vamp.73 days = 5 random = 4 }
			}
		}
		c_mourkain = { ROOT = { capital = PREV } }
		e_strygos = {
			grant_title = ROOT
			make_primary_title = yes
			add_law = succ_turkish_succession
			add_law = true_cognatic_succession
		}
	}
}

# vamp.71 Strygos restored by the Strigany
# A mortal Strigany restores Strygos (e_strygos_2), as in vamp.70.

	id = vamp.71
	desc = EVTDESCvamp71
	picture = GFX_evt_human_lord

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp71

			primary_title = {
				any_direct_de_jure_vassal_title = { de_jure_liege = e_strygos_2 }
				k_khernarch = { de_jure_liege = e_strygos_2 }
				k_strygos = { de_jure_liege = e_strygos_2 }
				k_badlands = { de_jure_liege = e_strygos_2 }
			}
			any_playable_ruler = {
				limit = { NOT = { character = ROOT } trait = lahmian_queen }

			}

			grant_title = ROOT
			make_primary_title = yes
			add_law = succ_turkish_succession
			add_law = true_cognatic_succession
		}
	}
}

# vamp.72 The Lahmians failed
# The Lahmian queen hears that Strygos has risen again and loses prestige.
# Scope: ROOT is the Lahmian queen, FROM the new emperor.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.72
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCvamp72
		trigger = { FROM = { NOT = { trait = vampire_strigoi_visible } } }
	}
	desc = { text = EVTDESCvamp72b trigger = { FROM = { trait = vampire_strigoi_visible } } }
	picture = GFX_evt_strigoi_lord
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp72
		prestige = -1500
	}
}

# vamp.73 Strygos reborn
# A Strigoi ruler may swear fealty to the new Strygos (always if a Strigoi
# rules it).
# Scope: ROOT is the Strigoi ruler, FROM the new emperor.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.73
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCvamp73
		trigger = { FROM = { NOT = { trait = vampire_strigoi_visible } } }
	}
	desc = { text = EVTDESCvamp73b trigger = { FROM = { trait = vampire_strigoi_visible } } }
	picture = GFX_evt_strigoi_lord
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp73
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { trait = vampire_strigoi_visible } }
			set_defacto_liege = e_strygos
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { NOT = { trait = vampire_strigoi_visible } } }
			set_defacto_liege = e_strygos_2
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = { factor = 0 FROM = { NOT = { trait = vampire_strigoi_visible } } }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBvamp73
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
	}
}

# vamp.74 The old ways
# The emperor of Strygos may take the old Strygan culture and faith. If so,
# Strigany provinces in Strygos slowly turn Strygan (vamp.75, vamp.76).
# Scope: ROOT is the emperor.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.74
	desc = EVTDESCvamp74
	picture = GFX_evt_wandering_strigany

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp74
		set_global_flag = strygos_culture_swapping
		culture = strygan
		capital_scope = { culture = strygan religion = vampiric }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { trait = vampire_strigoi_visible } }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBvamp74
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
}

# vamp.75 Strygan once more
# A Strigany province in Strygos takes up the old Strygan customs.
# Scope: ROOT is the province.
province_event = {
	id = vamp.75
	desc = EVTDESCvamp75
	picture = GFX_evt_wandering_strigany
	is_triggered_only = yes # vamp.77

	trigger = {
		has_global_flag = strygos_culture_swapping
		region = world_badlands_strygos
		culture = strigany
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp75
		culture = strygan
	}
}

# vamp.76 The Strigany return
# Strigany settlers come back to a province of Strygos held by a Strigany.
# Scope: ROOT is the province.
province_event = {
	id = vamp.76
	desc = EVTDESCvamp76
	picture = GFX_evt_wandering_strigany
	is_triggered_only = yes # vamp.77

	trigger = {
		has_global_flag = strygos_culture_swapping
		region = world_badlands_strygos
		NOT = { culture = strigany }
		NOT = { culture = strygan }
		owner = { culture_group = strigany_group }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp76
		culture = strigany
	}
}

# vamp.77 Strygos is resettled
# Hidden. Every five years each province of the emperor's realm in Strygos
# may turn Strygan (vamp.75) or Strigany (vamp.76), each about once in 60
# years.
# Scope: ROOT is the emperor of Strygos.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.77
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_five_year_pulse

	only_rulers = yes

	trigger = {
		has_global_flag = strygos_culture_swapping
		has_global_flag = strygos_reformed
		OR = { has_landed_title = e_strygos has_landed_title = e_strygos_2 }
	}

	immediate = {
		any_realm_province = {
			limit = { region = world_badlands_strygos culture = strigany }
			random = { chance = 8 province_event = { id = vamp.75 } }
		}
		any_realm_province = {
			limit = {
				region = world_badlands_strygos
				NOT = { culture = strigany }
				NOT = { culture = strygan }
				owner = { culture_group = strigany_group }
			}
			random = { chance = 8 province_event = { id = vamp.76 } }
		}
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Strigany caravans
###############################################################################

# vamp.80 A Strigany caravan
# A Strigany caravan sent by a decision asks to trade in a ruler's capital.
# Welcomed, its leader moves in and reports home (vamp.82); turned away, it
# goes home empty-handed (vamp.81).
# Scope: ROOT is the host, FROM the caravan leader, FROMFROM the sender.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.80
	desc = EVTDESCvamp80
	picture = GFX_evt_wandering_strigany

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = strigany_turned_away }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp80
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 75
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = zealous NOT = { religion_group = necromantic_group } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = charitable }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = trusting }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = paranoid }
		}
		capital_scope = { add_province_modifier = { modifier = strigany_caravan duration = -1 } }
		# To keep track of both temporary liege and caravan chief
		opinion = { who = FROM months = 10000 modifier = opinion_strigany_caravan_beacon2 }
		# To keep track of both temporary liege and caravan chief
		FROM = {
			opinion = { who = ROOT months = 10000 modifier = opinion_strigany_caravan_beacon3 }
		}
		FROM = { move_character = ROOT set_character_flag = strygany_caravan_master }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			FROMFROM = {
				# Your caravan has established a foothold
				letter_event = { id = vamp.82 days = 7 random = 4 }
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBvamp80
		ai_chance = { factor = 25 }
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = strigany_turned_away years = 2 }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			FROMFROM = {
				# Your caravan returned without any wares
				character_event = { id = vamp.81 days = 7 random = 4 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# vamp.81 Turned away
# The caravan came home with nothing.
# Scope: ROOT is the sender.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.81
	desc = EVTDESCvamp81
	picture = GFX_evt_wandering_strigany

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp81
	}
}

# vamp.82 A foothold
# The caravan has settled in; the sender gets a share and the season's trade
# is rolled: good (vamp.83), fair (vamp.86), bad (vamp.88), driven out
# (vamp.91) or a permanent settlement (vamp.94).
# Scope: ROOT is the sender, FROM the host, FROMFROM the caravan leader.
letter_event = {
	id = vamp.82
	desc = EVTDESCvamp82
	show_from_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp82
		wealth = 25
		hidden_tooltip = {
			FROMFROM = {
				random_list = {
					# the Strigany get a good deal
					15 = {
						modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROMFROM = { stewardship = 10 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROMFROM = { stewardship = 15 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROMFROM = { stewardship = 20 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROMFROM = { stewardship = 25 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROMFROM = { diplomacy = 10 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROMFROM = { trait = midas_touched } }
						character_event = { id = vamp.83 days = 170 random = 140 }
					}
					# the Strigany get a fair deal
					50 = { character_event = { id = vamp.86 days = 170 random = 140 } }
					# the Strigany upset locals
					25 = { character_event = { id = vamp.88 days = 170 random = 140 } }
					# the Strigany are driven out by angry locals
					10 = {
						modifier = {
							factor = 0.5
							FROM = {
								NOT = {
									capital_scope = { has_province_modifier = strigany_strife }
								}
							}
						}
						character_event = { id = vamp.91 days = 170 random = 140 }
					}
					# the Strigany are entrenched beyond repair
					5 = {
						modifier = {
							factor = 0
							FROM = { capital_scope = { has_empty_holding = no } }
						}
						modifier = {
							factor = 0
							NOT = {
								FROMFROM = {
									check_variable = { which = "strigany_presence_v" value = 15 }
								}
							}
						}
						modifier = { factor = 1.75 FROMFROM = { stewardship = 20 } }
						modifier = { factor = 2 FROMFROM = { trait = ambitious } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROMFROM = { trait = deceitful } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROMFROM = { trait = midas_touched } }
						character_event = { id = vamp.94 days = 170 random = 140 }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# vamp.83 A good season
# The caravan made a good profit and the Strigany presence grows.
# Scope: ROOT is the caravan leader, FROM the sender.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.83
	desc = EVTDESCvamp83
	picture = GFX_evt_wandering_strigany

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		liege = {
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = expelled_strigany }
			NOT = { top_liege = { has_character_modifier = expelled_strigany } }
			capital_scope = { has_province_modifier = strigany_caravan }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp83
		wealth = 75
		change_variable = { which = "strigany_presence_v" value = 1 }
		FROM = { letter_event = { id = vamp.84 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
}

# vamp.84 A good season (sender)
# The sender gets their share and keeps the caravan there (next season
# rolled again) or calls it home (vamp.85).
# Scope: ROOT is the sender, FROM the caravan leader.
letter_event = {
	id = vamp.84
	desc = EVTDESCvamp84

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp84
		wealth = 95
		hidden_tooltip = {
			FROM = {
				random_list = {
					15 = {
						modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { stewardship = 10 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { stewardship = 15 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { stewardship = 20 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { stewardship = 25 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { diplomacy = 10 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = midas_touched } }
						character_event = { id = vamp.83 days = 170 random = 140 }
					} # the Strigany get a good deal
					45 = { character_event = { id = vamp.86 days = 170 random = 140 } } # the Strigany get a fair deal
					25 = { character_event = { id = vamp.88 days = 170 random = 140 } } # the Strigany upset locals
					15 = {
						modifier = {
							factor = 0.5
							FROM = {
								liege = {
									NOT = {
										capital_scope = { has_province_modifier = strigany_strife }
									}
								}
							}
						}
						character_event = { id = vamp.91 days = 170 random = 140 }
					} # the Strigany are driven out by angry locals
					5 = {
						modifier = {
							factor = 0
							FROM = { capital_scope = { has_empty_holding = no } }
						}
						modifier = {
							factor = 0
							NOT = {
								FROM = {
									check_variable = { which = "strigany_presence_v" value = 15 }
								}
							}
						}
						modifier = {
							factor = 0
							FROM = { liege = { capital_scope = { has_empty_holding = no } } }
						}
						modifier = { factor = 1.75 FROM = { stewardship = 20 } }
						modifier = { factor = 2 FROM = { trait = ambitious } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = deceitful } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = midas_touched } }
						character_event = { id = vamp.94 days = 170 random = 140 }
					} # the Strigany are entrenched beyond repair
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBvamp84
		wealth = 95
		FROM = {
			clr_character_flag = strygany_caravan_master
			liege = {
				FROM = {
					remove_opinion = { who = PREV modifier = opinion_strigany_caravan_beacon2 }
				}
				character_event = { id = vamp.85 }
				capital_scope = { remove_province_modifier = strigany_caravan }
			}
		}
		FROM = { move_character = ROOT }
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

		prestige = -50
		capital_scope = { remove_province_modifier = strigany_caravan }
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
}

# vamp.94 A Strigany settlement
# The caravan has grown into a tribe holding in the host's capital and swears
# fealty to the sender.
# Scope: ROOT is the caravan leader, FROM the sender.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.94
	desc = EVTDESCvamp94
	picture = GFX_evt_wandering_strigany

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		liege = {
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = expelled_strigany }
			NOT = { top_liege = { has_character_modifier = expelled_strigany } }
			capital_scope = { has_province_modifier = strigany_caravan }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp94
		wealth = 150
		prestige = 150
		clr_character_flag = strygany_caravan_master
		liege = {
			ROOT = { remove_opinion = { who = PREV modifier = opinion_strigany_caravan_beacon2 } }
			character_event = { id = vamp.95 }
			capital_scope = { build_holding = { type = tribal holder = ROOT } }
		}
		set_variable = { which = "strigany_presence_v" value = 0 }
		ROOT = { set_defacto_liege = FROM }
		FROM = { letter_event = { id = vamp.96 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
}

# vamp.97 A caravan leader dies
# Hidden. A dead Strigany caravan leader's caravan leaves the host's capital.
# Scope: ROOT is the dead caravan leader.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.97
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_death
	culture = strigany

	trigger = {
		has_any_opinion_modifier = opinion_strigany_caravan_beacon3
		trait = creature_human
	}

	immediate = {
		any_playable_ruler = {
			limit = {
				has_opinion_modifier = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_strigany_caravan_beacon2 }
			}

			capital_scope = { remove_province_modifier = strigany_caravan }
		}
	}
}

# vamp.98 A caravan host dies
# Hidden. A dead host's caravans pass to the heir.
# Scope: ROOT is the dead host.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.98
	hide_window = yes

	only_rulers = yes

	trigger = {
		has_any_opinion_modifier = opinion_strigany_caravan_beacon2
		any_courtier = {
			ROOT = {
				has_opinion_modifier = { who = PREV modifier = opinion_strigany_caravan_beacon2 }
			}
		}
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_death

	immediate = {
		current_heir = {
			ROOT = {
				any_courtier = {
					limit = { has_character_flag = strygany_caravan_master }
					reverse_opinion = {
						who = PREVPREV
						modifier = opinion_strigany_caravan_beacon2
						months = 10000
					}
					opinion = {
						who = PREVPREV
						modifier = opinion_strigany_caravan_beacon3
						months = 10000
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Necrarchs
###############################################################################

# vamp.100 Subjects for study
# A Necrarch on a thrall raid takes a capital: kill everyone (the owner and
# their top liege are told, vamp.101 and vamp.102), take them as test
# subjects (vamp.103) or leave them.
# Scope: ROOT is the Necrarch, FROM the province.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.100
	desc = EVTDESCvamp100
	picture = GFX_evt_slaves

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_siege_over_winner

	religion = nagashi

	trigger = {
		NOT = { location = { has_province_modifier = province_slave_raid } }
		NOT = { location = { has_province_modifier = province_scourged } }
		NOT = { location = { has_province_modifier = province_slave_raid_chaos_dwarf } }
		any_war = { attacker = { character = ROOT } using_cb = necrarch_thrall_raid }
		FROM = {
			is_capital = yes
			NOT = { culture = nehekharan }
			owner = { is_undead_trigger = no }
		} #only against the living
	}

	option = { #kill them all!
		name = EVTOPTAvamp100
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { owner = { top_liege = { NOT = { character = PREV } } } } }
			FROM = { owner = { top_liege = { character_event = { id = vamp.102 } } } }
		}
		FROM = { owner = { character_event = { id = vamp.101 } } }
		FROM = { set_province_flag = flag_province_scourged }
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					OR = {
						any_neighbor_province = { is_tribal = yes }
						has_province_flag = flag_province_scourged
						NOT = { num_of_settlements = 2 }
					}
					NOT = { is_nomadic = yes }
				}
			}
			random = { chance = 1 FROM = { convert_to = TRIBAL } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = { location = { NOT = { has_province_modifier = province_scourged } } }
			}
			FROM = {
				location = { add_province_modifier = { name = province_scourged duration = 2400 } }
			}
		}

		wealth = 125
		prestige = 50
		piety = 250
		change_variable = { which = "sample_counter" value = 1 }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 25
			modifier = { factor = 1.75 ROOT = { OR = { trait = lunatic_3 trait = lunatic_2 } } }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = mystic }
		}
	}
	option = { #take them all!
		name = EVTOPTBvamp100
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { owner = { top_liege = { NOT = { character = PREV } } } } }
			FROM = { owner = { top_liege = { character_event = { id = vamp.103 } } } }
		}
		FROM = { owner = { character_event = { id = vamp.103 } } }
		wealth = 25
		prestige = 10
		piety = 10
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = { location = { NOT = { has_province_modifier = province_slave_raid } } }
			}
			FROM = {
				location = {
					add_province_modifier = { name = province_slave_raid duration = 2400 }
				}
			}
		}
		change_variable = { which = "sample_counter" value = 3 }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 75
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				ROOT = { NOT = { trait = possessed trait = lunatic_3 trait = lunatic_2 } }
			}
			modifier = { factor = 1.2 trait = kind }
		}
	}
	option = { #I don't care about this province
		name = EVTOPTCvamp100
		piety = -25
		prestige = -25
		if = { limit = { trait = cruel } random = { chance = 1 remove_trait = cruel } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.2
				ROOT = { NOR = { trait = possessed trait = lunatic_3 trait = lunatic_2 } }
			}
			modifier = { factor = 1.2 trait = kind }
		}
	}
}

# vamp.105 Experiments
# A Necrarch with enough subjects studies them (vamp.106 to vamp.109) or makes
# servants of them (vamp.110 to vamp.113).
# Scope: ROOT is the Necrarch.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.105
	desc = EVTDESCvamp105
	picture = "GFX_evt_lab"

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_rulers = yes

	option = { #Bring forward your studies
		name = EVTOPTAvamp105
		clr_character_flag = experimenting_necromancy
		change_variable = { which = "sample_counter" value = -5 }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				5 = { character_event = { id = vamp.106 } }
				15 = { character_event = { id = vamp.107 } }
				35 = { character_event = { id = vamp.108 } }
				45 = { character_event = { id = vamp.109 } }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 20 }
	}
	option = { #Create servants
		name = EVTOPTBvamp105
		clr_character_flag = experimenting_necromancy
		change_variable = { which = "sample_counter" value = -4 }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				5 = { character_event = { id = vamp.110 } }
				15 = { character_event = { id = vamp.111 } }
				35 = { character_event = { id = vamp.112 } }
				45 = { character_event = { id = vamp.113 } }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 80 }
	}
	option = { #Nothing
		name = EVTOPTCvamp105
		clr_character_flag = experimenting_necromancy
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Jade vampires
###############################################################################

# vamp.120 The Jade decay
# Every five years a Jade vampire must gaze into a Dark Crystal (their own or
# their top liege's, vamp.121) or decay a step: jade_degradation 1 to 4,
# madness and lost skills.
# Scope: ROOT is the Jade vampire.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.120
	desc = EVTDESCvamp120
	picture = GFX_evt_vampire_jades

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_five_year_pulse

	min_age = 40

	trigger = {
		trait = vampire_jade_visible
	}

	option = { #Use the crystal
		name = EVTOPTAvamp120
		trigger = {
			is_incapacitated_trigger = no
			OR = {
				has_artifact = dark_crystal
				top_liege = {
					has_artifact = dark_crystal
					NOT = { has_character_flag = forbidden_jade_crystal }
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = vamp.121 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 90
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = paranoid }
			modifier = { factor = 0.85 trait = proud }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBvamp120
		if = {
			limit = {
				top_liege = {
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
					has_character_flag = forbidden_jade_crystal
				}
			}
			top_liege = { ROOT = { opinion = { who = PREV modifier = opinion_forced_abstinence } } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = lore_heavens }
			remove_trait = lore_heavens
			remove_character_modifier = magic_power_1
			remove_character_modifier = magic_power_2
			remove_character_modifier = magic_power_3
			remove_character_modifier = magic_power_4
		}

		if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = jade_degradation_4 }
			change_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = 3 }
			random = { chance = 20 change_diplomacy = -1 change_learning = -1 }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = jade_degradation_3 }
			change_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = 1 }
			remove_character_modifier = jade_degradation_3
			add_character_modifier = { name = jade_degradation_4 duration = -1 }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = jade_degradation_2 }
			change_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = 1 }
			remove_character_modifier = jade_degradation_2
			add_character_modifier = { name = jade_degradation_3 duration = -1 }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = jade_degradation_1 }
			remove_character_modifier = jade_degradation_1
			add_character_modifier = { name = jade_degradation_2 duration = -1 }
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				NOR = {
					has_character_modifier = jade_degradation_1
					has_character_modifier = jade_degradation_2
					has_character_modifier = jade_degradation_3
					has_character_modifier = jade_degradation_4
				}
			}
			add_character_modifier = { name = jade_degradation_1 duration = -1 }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 10 }
	}
}

# vamp.121 Basking in the crystal
# The crystal heals the decay and may give Lore of Heavens. A ruler may then
# receive a demand or a vision from the Dragon-Emperor (vamp.122).
# Scope: ROOT is the Jade vampire.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.121
	desc = EVTDESCvamp121
	picture = GFX_evt_vampire_jade_basking

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp121
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOR = {
					has_character_modifier = jade_degradation_1
					has_character_modifier = jade_degradation_2
					has_character_modifier = jade_degradation_3
					has_character_modifier = jade_degradation_4
					has_character_modifier = magic_power_1
					has_character_modifier = magic_power_2
					has_character_modifier = magic_power_3
					has_character_modifier = magic_power_4
				}
			}
			add_trait = lore_heavens
			add_character_modifier = { name = magic_power_2 duration = -1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = jade_degradation_1 }
			remove_character_modifier = jade_degradation_1
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = jade_degradation_2 }
			remove_character_modifier = jade_degradation_2
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = jade_degradation_3 }
			change_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = -1 }
			remove_character_modifier = jade_degradation_3
			add_character_modifier = { name = jade_degradation_1 duration = -1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = jade_degradation_4 }
			change_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = -2 }
			remove_character_modifier = jade_degradation_4
			add_character_modifier = { name = jade_degradation_2 duration = -1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { higher_tier_than = BARON }
			hidden_tooltip = { random = { chance = 70 character_event = { id = vamp.122 } } }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
}

# vamp.122 The Dragon-Emperor's will
# Hidden. Rolls what the crystal shows: a trinket demanded (vamp.123), a
# quest (vamp.124), visions that teach or break the mind (vamp.125 to
# vamp.138), or relief from madness (vamp.139) and mutation (vamp.140).
# Scope: ROOT is the Jade ruler.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.122
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			5 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.5 religion = draconite }
				character_event = { id = vamp.123 days = 5 random = 5 }
			} # A strange and valuable trinket is requested and shipped
			6 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.5 religion = draconite }
				character_event = { id = vamp.124 days = 5 random = 5 }
			} # Forced to go questing
			3 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.1 is_living_human_trigger = yes }
				character_event = { id = vamp.125 days = 5 random = 5 }
			} # Glimpsed at the Heavens - magic
			5 = { character_event = { id = vamp.126 days = 5 random = 5 } } # Increased awareness - learning
			5 = { character_event = { id = vamp.127 days = 5 random = 5 } } # Looked into Xa Feng's dreams - tech
			2 = { character_event = { id = vamp.128 days = 5 random = 5 } } # Overwhelmed by knowledge - incapable
			5 = { character_event = { id = vamp.129 days = 5 random = 5 } } # Stared for too long into the Heavens - learning malus
			5 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { is_living_human_trigger = yes } }
				character_event = { id = vamp.130 days = 5 random = 5 }
			} # Gone insane
			5 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { is_living_human_trigger = yes } }
				modifier = { factor = 0 religion = draconite }
				character_event = { id = vamp.131 days = 5 random = 5 }
			} # Died
			5 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { is_living_human_trigger = yes } }
				modifier = { factor = 0 religion = draconite }
				character_event = { id = vamp.132 days = 5 random = 5 }
			} # Converted
			5 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.1 NOT = { religion = draconite } }
				character_event = { id = vamp.133 days = 5 random = 5 }
			} #Temporary insight - Diplomacy
			6 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.1 NOT = { religion = draconite } }
				character_event = { id = vamp.134 days = 5 random = 5 }
			} #Temporary insight - Martial
			5 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.1 NOT = { religion = draconite } }
				character_event = { id = vamp.135 days = 5 random = 5 }
			} #Temporary insight - Stewardship
			6 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.1 NOT = { religion = draconite } }
				character_event = { id = vamp.136 days = 5 random = 5 }
			} #Temporary insight - Intrigue
			6 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.1 NOT = { religion = draconite } }
				character_event = { id = vamp.137 days = 5 random = 5 }
			} #Temporary insight - Learning
			1 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { religion = draconite } }
				modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { piety = 0 } }
				character_event = { id = vamp.138 days = 5 random = 5 }
			} #Temporary insight - Piety
			6 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					NOR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 }
				}
				character_event = { id = vamp.139 days = 5 random = 5 }
			} #Clarity of mind
			6 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 is_mutated_trigger = yes }
				character_event = { id = vamp.140 days = 5 random = 5 }
			} #Clarity of body
			13 = { modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { religion = draconite } } }
		}
	}
}

	}

# vamp.124 A summons
# The vampire is compelled to leave on a quest (questing); it ends in
# vamp.141, vamp.142 or vamp.143.
# Scope: ROOT is the Jade ruler.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.124
	desc = EVTDESCvamp124
	picture = GFX_evt_heavenly_dragon

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp124
		add_trait = questing
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				65 = { character_event = { id = vamp.141 days = 150 random = 350 } } # Nothing
				30 = { character_event = { id = vamp.142 days = 150 random = 350 } } # Badly wounded
				5 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0 age = 500 }
					character_event = { id = vamp.143 days = 150 random = 350 }
				} # Killed
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
}

# vamp.125 A glimpse of the Heavens
# Magic power grows a step (Lore of Heavens).
# Scope: ROOT is the Jade ruler.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.125
	desc = EVTDESCvamp125
	picture = GFX_evt_vampire_jade_basking

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp125
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = mutate.5 } } #lunacy counter
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = magic_power_4 } change_learning = 3 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = magic_power_3 }
			remove_character_modifier = magic_power_3
			add_character_modifier = { name = magic_power_4 duration = -1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = magic_power_2 }
			remove_character_modifier = magic_power_2
			add_character_modifier = { name = magic_power_3 duration = -1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = magic_power_1 }
			remove_character_modifier = magic_power_1
			add_character_modifier = { name = magic_power_2 duration = -1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOR = {
					has_character_modifier = magic_power_1
					has_character_modifier = magic_power_2
					has_character_modifier = magic_power_3
					has_character_modifier = magic_power_4
				}
			}
			add_trait = lore_heavens
			add_character_modifier = { name = magic_power_2 duration = -1 }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
}

# vamp.139 Clarity of mind
# Madness recedes (lunatic_counter -10).
# Scope: ROOT is the Jade ruler.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.139
	desc = EVTDESCvamp139
	picture = GFX_evt_heavenly_dragon

	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = {
		change_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = -10 }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp139
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = 1 } } }
			remove_trait = lunatic_1
			remove_trait = lunatic_2
			remove_trait = lunatic_3
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { check_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = 10 } }
				check_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = 5 }
			}
			remove_trait = lunatic_2
			remove_trait = lunatic_3
			add_trait = lunatic_1
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { check_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = 20 } }
				check_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = 10 }
			}
			remove_trait = lunatic_1
			remove_trait = lunatic_3
			add_trait = lunatic_2
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
}

# vamp.140 Clarity of body
# Mutations burn away (mutation_counter -10), at the cost of a wound.
# Scope: ROOT is the Jade ruler.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.140
	desc = EVTDESCvamp140
	picture = GFX_evt_heavenly_dragon

	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = {
		change_variable = { which = "mutation_counter" value = -10 }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp140
		add_trait = wounded
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { which = "mutation_counter" value = 1 } } }
			remove_trait = mutation_minor
			remove_trait = mutation_major
			remove_trait = mutation_massive
			remove_trait = mut_spines
			remove_trait = mut_horns
			remove_trait = mut_scales
			remove_trait = mut_thirdeye
			remove_trait = mut_tongue
			remove_trait = mut_crests
			remove_trait = mut_doublehead
			remove_trait = mut_beak
			remove_trait = mut_teeth
			remove_trait = mut_eyes
			remove_trait = mut_bigeyes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { check_variable = { which = "mutation_counter" value = 10 } }
				check_variable = { which = "mutation_counter" value = 5 }
			}
			remove_trait = mutation_major
			remove_trait = mutation_massive
			add_trait = mutation_minor
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { check_variable = { which = "mutation_counter" value = 20 } }
				check_variable = { which = "mutation_counter" value = 10 }
			}
			remove_trait = mutation_minor
			remove_trait = mutation_massive
			add_trait = mutation_major
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
}

# vamp.145 The hypnotic stare
# A Jade vampire's gaze ensnares the target: usually friendship and a hold
# over them; the clever and the magically gifted may resist.
# Scope: ROOT is the target, FROMFROM the Jade vampire.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.145
	desc = EVTDESCvamp145
	picture = GFX_evt_vampire_jade_hypnosis

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp145
		prisoner = no
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				75 = {
					opinion = { who = FROMFROM modifier = opinion_ensnared_me }
					if = { limit = { NOT = { num_of_friends = 5 } } add_friend = FROMFROM }
				}
				25 = {
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_character_modifier = magic_power_1 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.75 has_character_modifier = magic_power_2 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.95 has_character_modifier = magic_power_3 }
					modifier = { factor = 2 has_character_modifier = magic_power_4 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = magic_potential_1 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.35 trait = magic_potential_2 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.45 trait = magic_potential_3 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = magic_potential_4 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.35 trait = lore_high_magic }
					modifier = { factor = 1.35 trait = lore_dark_magic }
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = imbecile }
					modifier = { factor = 0.65 trait = idiot }
					modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = slow }
					modifier = { factor = 0.85 trait = dumb }
					modifier = { factor = 0.95 trait = dim }
					modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = bright }
					modifier = { factor = 1.35 trait = smart }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = quick }
					modifier = { factor = 1.75 trait = brilliant }
					modifier = { factor = 2 trait = genius }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = creature_elf }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = creature_dwarf }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = creature_skink }
					modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = creature_saurus }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 any_rival = { character = FROMFROM } }
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
}

# vamp.146 Fed upon
# The Jade have fed too much in a necromantic province (jade_depopulation).
# Scope: ROOT is the province.
province_event = {
	id = vamp.146
	desc = EVTDESCvamp146
	picture = GFX_evt_vampire_jade_eyes
	is_triggered_only = yes # vamp.148

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_province_modifier = jade_mayhem }
		NOT = { culture = nehekharan }
		religion_group = necromantic_group
		owner = { trait = vampire_jade_visible }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp146
		if = {
			limit = { capital_holding = { has_building = ca_jade_stock_1 } }
			capital_holding = { remove_building = ca_jade_stock_1 }
		}
		add_province_modifier = { name = jade_depopulation duration = 900 stacking = yes }
	}
}

	}
