# vamp.148 The Jade feed
# Hidden. Every five years each province of a Jade vampire ruler may be fed
# upon (vamp.146, about once in 60 years) or resist (vamp.147, about once in
# 40 years). Small realms and many Jade courtiers make it likelier.
# Scope: ROOT is the Jade ruler.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.148
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_five_year_pulse

	only_rulers = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = vampire_jade_visible
		higher_tier_than = BARON
	}

	immediate = {
		any_demesne_province = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_province_modifier = jade_mayhem } NOT = { culture = nehekharan } }
			if = {
				limit = { religion_group = necromantic_group }
				random = {
					chance = 8
					modifier = { factor = 1.05 is_capital = yes }
					modifier = { factor = 0.67 owner = { war = yes } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.33 has_province_modifier = province_scourged }
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.33
						has_province_modifier = province_slave_raid_chaos_dwarf
					}
					modifier = { factor = 1.33 has_province_modifier = province_slave_raid }
					modifier = { factor = 2 NOT = { owner = { top_liege = { realm_size = 20 } } } }
					modifier = { factor = 0.67 owner = { top_liege = { realm_size = 80 } } }
					modifier = { factor = 0.67 owner = { top_liege = { realm_size = 120 } } }
					modifier = { factor = 0.67 owner = { top_liege = { realm_size = 150 } } }
					modifier = { factor = 0.67 owner = { top_liege = { realm_size = 200 } } }
					modifier = {
						factor = 2
						owner = {
							top_liege = {
								any_courtier = { trait = vampire_jade_visible count = 5 }
							}
						}
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 2
						owner = {
							top_liege = {
								any_courtier = { trait = vampire_jade_visible count = 10 }
							}
						}
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 2
						owner = {
							top_liege = {
								any_courtier = { trait = vampire_jade_visible count = 15 }
							}
						}
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 2
						owner = {
							top_liege = {
								any_courtier = { trait = vampire_jade_visible count = 20 }
							}
						}
					}
					province_event = { id = vamp.146 }
				}
			}
			else = {
				random = {
					chance = 12
					modifier = { factor = 0.25 culture_group = lizardman_group }
					modifier = { factor = 0.8 is_capital = yes }
					modifier = { factor = 0.2 has_province_modifier = province_scourged }
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.36
						has_province_modifier = province_slave_raid_chaos_dwarf
					}
					modifier = { factor = 0.36 has_province_modifier = province_slave_raid }
					modifier = { factor = 2 NOT = { owner = { top_liege = { realm_size = 20 } } } }
					modifier = { factor = 0.67 owner = { top_liege = { realm_size = 80 } } }
					modifier = { factor = 0.67 owner = { top_liege = { realm_size = 120 } } }
					modifier = { factor = 0.67 owner = { top_liege = { realm_size = 150 } } }
					modifier = { factor = 0.67 owner = { top_liege = { realm_size = 200 } } }
					province_event = { id = vamp.147 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# vamp.150 The great feast
# With a decision the Jade round up a province: partly cleansed (vamp.151),
# emptied (vamp.152) or risen in revolt (vamp.153).
# Scope: ROOT is the Jade ruler, FROM the title.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.150
	desc = EVTDESCvamp150
	picture = GFX_evt_vampire_jade_eyes
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp150
		if = { limit = { ai = no } wealth = -85 }
		else = { wealth = -35 }
		prestige = 100
		piety = 100
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				40 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.5
						FROM = { location = { has_province_modifier = jade_mayhem } }
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.5
						FROM = { location = { has_province_modifier = province_scourged } }
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.5
						FROM = {
							location = { has_province_modifier = province_slave_raid_chaos_dwarf }
						}
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.5
						FROM = { location = { has_province_modifier = province_slave_raid } }
					}
					character_event = { id = vamp.151 days = 25 random = 50 }
				} #partially cleansed
				40 = { character_event = { id = vamp.152 days = 25 random = 50 } } #entirely cleansed
				20 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.5
						FROM = { location = { has_province_modifier = jade_mayhem } }
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.5
						FROM = { location = { has_province_modifier = province_scourged } }
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.5
						FROM = {
							location = { has_province_modifier = province_slave_raid_chaos_dwarf }
						}
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.5
						FROM = { location = { has_province_modifier = province_slave_raid } }
					}
					character_event = { id = vamp.153 days = 25 random = 50 }
				} #peasant uprising
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBvamp150
		prestige = -50
		remove_trait = questing
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

# vamp.151 Partly cleansed
# The province is fed upon and loses a building (jade_depopulation); another
# province recovers.
# Scope: ROOT is the Jade ruler, FROMFROM the title.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.151
	desc = EVTDESCvamp151
	picture = GFX_evt_vampire_jade_eyes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp151
		remove_trait = questing
		if = {
			limit = { FROMFROM = { has_building = ca_jade_stock_1 } }
			FROMFROM = { remove_building = ca_jade_stock_1 }
		}
		FROMFROM = {
			location = {
				destroy_random_building = yes
				add_province_modifier = { name = jade_depopulation duration = 500 stacking = yes }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				capital_scope = {
					has_province_modifier = jade_depopulation
					religion_group = necromantic_group
					NOT = { province = FROMFROM }
				}
			}
			capital_scope = { remove_province_modifier = jade_depopulation }
		}
		else = {
			random_demesne_province = {
				limit = {
					has_province_modifier = jade_depopulation
					religion_group = necromantic_group
					NOT = { province = FROMFROM }
				}
				remove_province_modifier = jade_depopulation
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
}

# vamp.155 Raiders feed
# A Jade ruler takes a civilised capital: drain the people, up to five
# courtiers of the court and a building (owner and top liege are told,
# vamp.156 and vamp.157).
# Scope: ROOT is the Jade ruler, FROM the province.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.155
	desc = EVTDESCvamp155
	picture = GFX_evt_vampire_jade_eyes

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_siege_over_winner, random list

	trigger = {
		higher_tier_than = BARON
		trait = vampire_jade_visible
		FROM = { NOT = { location = { has_province_modifier = province_slave_raid } } }
		FROM = { NOT = { location = { has_province_modifier = province_scourged } } }
		FROM = { NOT = { location = { has_province_modifier = province_slave_raid_chaos_dwarf } } }
		FROM = { NOT = { location = { culture = nehekharan } } }
		FROM = { is_tribal = no is_nomadic = no is_capital = yes } #only against civilized places
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp155
		if = {
			limit = {
				capital_scope = {
					has_province_modifier = jade_depopulation
					religion_group = necromantic_group
					NOT = { province = FROMFROM }
				}
			}
			capital_scope = { remove_province_modifier = jade_depopulation }
		}
		else = {
			random_demesne_province = {
				limit = {
					has_province_modifier = jade_depopulation
					religion_group = necromantic_group
					NOT = { province = FROMFROM }
				}
				remove_province_modifier = jade_depopulation
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					owner = {
						top_liege = { NOT = { character = PREV } NOT = { character = ROOT } }
					}
				}
			}
			FROM = { owner = { top_liege = { character_event = { id = vamp.157 } } } }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { owner = { NOT = { character = ROOT } } } }
			FROM = { owner = { character_event = { id = vamp.156 } } }
		}
		FROM = { set_province_flag = flag_province_scourged }
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = { location = { NOT = { has_province_modifier = province_slave_raid } } }
			}
			FROM = {
				location = { add_province_modifier = { name = province_slave_raid duration = 800 } }
			}
		}

		wealth = 125
		prestige = 50
		piety = 50
		hidden_tooltip = {
			FROM = { destroy_random_building = yes holder_scope = { vamp_jade_feast_effect = yes } }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBvamp155
		piety = -25
		prestige = -25
		if = { limit = { trait = cruel } random = { chance = 1 remove_trait = cruel } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

# vamp.160 A Dark Crystal found
# A duke or higher with the Dark Crystal ambition, holding Tlaxtlan, Calxi or
# Mutiris, finds a crystal there. Dragon God worshippers are far likelier.
# Players with an interest are told (vamp.161). Each county yields one crystal
# only (tlaxtlan/calxi/mutiris_crystal_found, which also end the ambition).
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.160
	desc = { text = EVTDESCvamp160 trigger = { religion = draconite trait = vampire_jade_visible } }
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCvamp160b
		trigger = { trait = vampire_jade_visible NOT = { religion = draconite } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCvamp160c
		trigger = { religion = draconite NOT = { trait = vampire_jade_visible } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCvamp160d
		trigger = { NOR = { religion = draconite trait = vampire_jade_visible } }
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_vampire_dark_crystal

	capable_only = yes
	only_rulers = yes
	prisoner = no
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_decade_pulse, random list

	trigger = {
		higher_tier_than = COUNT
		is_incapacitated_trigger = no
		has_ambition = obj_acquire_dark_crystal
		NOT = { has_artifact = dark_crystal }
		OR = {
			AND = {
				has_landed_title = c_tlaxtlan
				NOT = { has_global_flag = tlaxtlan_crystal_found }
			}
			AND = { has_landed_title = c_calxi NOT = { has_global_flag = calxi_crystal_found } }
			AND = { has_landed_title = c_mutiris NOT = { has_global_flag = mutiris_crystal_found } }
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1
		modifier = { factor = 1.15 trait = paranoid }
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = diligent }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = genius }
		modifier = { factor = 1.15 stewardship = 20 }
		modifier = { factor = 1.15 learning = 20 }
		modifier = { factor = 16 religion = draconite }
		modifier = { factor = 0.15 independent = no }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai = yes }
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			capital_scope = {
				province_id = 1758 # Tlaxtlan
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.25
			capital_scope = {
				province_id = 2420 # Mutiris
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.15
			capital_scope = {
				province_id = 1074 # Calxi
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp160
		add_artifact = dark_crystal
		prestige = 500
		# Each county holds one crystal
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_landed_title = c_tlaxtlan
				NOT = { has_global_flag = tlaxtlan_crystal_found }
			}
			set_global_flag = tlaxtlan_crystal_found
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_landed_title = c_calxi NOT = { has_global_flag = calxi_crystal_found } }
			set_global_flag = calxi_crystal_found
		}
		else = { set_global_flag = mutiris_crystal_found }
		any_playable_ruler = {
			limit = {
				higher_tier_than = COUNT
				ai = no
				OR = {
					has_artifact = dark_crystal
					trait = vampire_jade_visible
					learning = 20
					religion = draconite
				}
			}
			character_event = { id = vamp.161 days = 2 random = 10 }
		}
	}
}

		any_war = { limit = { defender = { character = FROM } } end_war = invalid }
		FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.171 days = 1 random = 5 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBvamp170
		piety = -350
		FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.172 days = 1 random = 5 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 modifier = { factor = 0 lower_tier_than = EMPEROR } }
	}
}

# vamp.172 The goblins march on
# The goblins ignored the warning.

	picture = GFX_evt_goblin_shaman

# vamp.180 The Shadowgate collapses
# If the Shadowrealm passes to someone who is neither Mahtmasi nor a
# necromancer, the title is destroyed and the Shadowmen faith loses
# authority. The other Bayaji rulers hear of it (vamp.181).
# Scope: ROOT is the new holder, FROM the title.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.180
	desc = EVTDESCvamp180
	picture = GFX_evt_wasai_explosion

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_new_holder, on_new_holder_inheritance, on_new_holder_usurpation

	only_rulers = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { title = k_shadowrealm }
		NOT = { trait = vampire_mahtmasi_visible }
		NOT = { trait = lore_necromancy }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp180
		k_shadowrealm = { destroy_landed_title = THIS }
		religion_authority = { modifier = shadowgate_collapsed years = 3000 }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			activate_title = { title = k_shadowrealm status = no }

			any_playable_ruler = {
				limit = { higher_tier_than = BARON religion_group = ormazd_gods_group }
				character_event = { id = vamp.181 days = 5 random = 15 }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
}

		religion = akghan
		ai_chance = { factor = 60 }
	}

# vamp.182 The Big Bokor is defeated
# Hidden. A Big Bokor who loses a war, while Shadowmen still hold office, may
# be pressed to abdicate (vamp.183).
# Scope: ROOT is the defender, FROM the attacker.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.182
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_war_ended_defeat

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			FROM = { has_landed_title = k_shadowrealm }
			ROOT = { has_landed_title = k_shadowrealm }
		}
		any_playable_ruler = { any_courtier = { has_minor_title = title_shadowman } }
	}

	only_rulers = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_landed_title = k_shadowrealm } }
			FROM = {
				random_list = {
					10 = { character_event = { id = vamp.183 } }
					90 = {
						modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = cruel }
						modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = ambitious }
						modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = greedy }
						modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = wroth }
						modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = impaler }
						modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = lunatic_3 }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_landed_title = k_shadowrealm } }
			ROOT = {
				random_list = {
					20 = { character_event = { id = vamp.183 } }
					80 = {
						modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = cruel }
						modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = ambitious }
						modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = greedy }
						modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = wroth }
						modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = impaler }
						modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = lunatic_3 }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# vamp.185 Shadow servants
# A Mahtmasi sends shadow servants to speed up a voodoo doll or murder plot
# (vamp.188 or vamp.189).
# Scope: ROOT is the plotter.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.185
	desc = EVTDESCvamp185
	picture = GFX_evt_mahtmasi_summoning
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp185
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_plot = plot_make_voodoo_doll }
				plot_target_char = { character_event = { id = vamp.188 days = 27 } }
				character_event = { id = vamp.186 days = 50 } # Clear the shadow flag
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse } }
				plot_target_char = { character_event = { id = vamp.189 days = 27 } }
				character_event = { id = vamp.186 days = 50 } # Clear the shadow flag
			}
		}
	}
}

# vamp.188 The shadows steal
# The shadow servants try to steal something of the target's for the voodoo doll
# doll, like vamp.205.
# Scope: ROOT is the target, FROM the plotter.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.188
	desc = EVTDESCvamp205
	picture = GFX_evt_feast
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		prisoner = no

		# Is the plot still in motion?
		OR = {
			FROM = { has_plot = plot_make_voodoo_doll plot_target_char = { character = ROOT } }
			FROMFROM = { has_plot = plot_make_voodoo_doll plot_target_char = { character = ROOT } }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		save_event_target_as = target_victim
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp205 # Does not notice weird behavior
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					AND = { trait = paranoid random = 33 }
					AND = { trait = arabyan_scholar random = 40 }
					AND = { trait = deceitful random = 50 }
					AND = {
						religion = shadowmen #Knows the danger
						random = 60
					}
					AND = {
						religion = akghan #Knows the danger
						random = 60
					}
					AND = { trait = trusting random = 90 }
					AND = {
						NOT = { trait = paranoid }
						NOT = { trait = trusting }
						NOT = { trait = deceitful }
						NOT = { religion = shadowmen }
						NOT = { trait = arabyan_scholar }
						NOT = { religion = akghan }
						random = 75
					}
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				25 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0 FROM = { has_character_flag = using_shadow_plot } }
					hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.213 days = 1 } } }
					character_event = { id = vamp.206 days = 1 } # Object stolen, he notices
				}
				75 = {
					hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.214 days = 1 } } }
					character_event = { id = vamp.207 days = 1 } # Object stolen, he doesn't notices
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBvamp205 # Notices weird movement
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = { trait = paranoid random = 33 }
				AND = { trait = arabyan_scholar random = 40 }
				AND = { trait = deceitful random = 50 }
				AND = {
					religion = shadowmen #Knows the danger
					random = 60
				}
				AND = {
					religion = akghan #Knows the danger
					random = 60
				}
				AND = { trait = trusting random = 90 }
				AND = {
					NOT = { trait = paranoid }
					NOT = { trait = trusting }
					NOT = { trait = deceitful }
					NOT = { trait = arabyan_scholar }
					NOT = { religion = shadowmen }
					NOT = { religion = akghan }
					random = 75
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0 FROM = { has_character_flag = using_shadow_plot } }
					hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.215 days = 1 } } }
					character_event = { id = vamp.208 days = 1 } # Lives, plotter discovered
				}
				50 = {
					hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.216 days = 1 } } }
					character_event = { id = vamp.209 days = 1 } # Lives, plotter unknown
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# vamp.189 The shadows poison
# The shadow servants try to poison the target of a murder plot, using the
# murder plot's own outcomes.
# Scope: ROOT is the target, FROM the plotter.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.189
	desc = EVTDESCvamp189
	picture = GFX_evt_feast
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		prisoner = no

		# Is the plot still in motion?
		OR = {
			FROM = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
			}
			FROMFROM = {
				OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
				plot_target_char = { character = ROOT }
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		save_event_target_as = target_victim
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp189 # Drinks the wine
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					AND = { trait = paranoid random = 33 }
					AND = { trait = temperate random = 50 }
					AND = { trait = gluttonous random = 90 }
					AND = {
						NOT = { trait = temperate }
						NOT = { trait = gluttonous }
						NOT = { trait = paranoid }
						random = 75
					}
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				25 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0 FROM = { has_character_flag = using_shadow_plot } }
					hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = 8163 days = 1 } } }
					character_event = { id = 8156 days = 1 } # Dies, murderer discovered
				}
				75 = {
					hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = 8164 days = 1 } } }
					character_event = { id = 8157 days = 1 } # Dies, murderer unknown
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBvamp189 # Discovers the poison
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = { trait = paranoid random = 33 }
				AND = { trait = temperate random = 50 }
				AND = { trait = gluttonous random = 90 }
				AND = {
					NOT = { trait = temperate }
					NOT = { trait = gluttonous }
					NOT = { trait = paranoid }
					random = 75
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0 FROM = { has_character_flag = using_shadow_plot } }
					hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = 8165 days = 1 } } }
					character_event = { id = 8158 days = 1 } # Lives, murderer discovered
				}
				50 = {
					hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = 8166 days = 1 } } }
					character_event = { id = 8159 days = 1 } # Lives, murderer unknown
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# vamp.200 The voodoo doll: the plotter's chance
# The head of a voodoo doll plot who is with the target sees a chance to steal
# something of theirs: backed by the plot, paid for, or done by their own
# cunning (learning). The theft comes a month later (vamp.205).
# Scope: ROOT is the plotter.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.200
	desc = EVTDESCvamp200
	picture = GFX_evt_whispers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	is_part_of_plot = yes

	trigger = {
		has_plot = plot_make_voodoo_doll
		plot_target_char = {
			age = 16
			prisoner = no
			at_location = ROOT # The Plot Head needs to be at the target's location to get a chance
			NOT = { trait = in_hiding }
			NOT = { trait = questing }
			NOT = { trait = blood_questing }
			NOT = { trait = monster_hunting }
		}
		NOT = { has_character_flag = murder_in_motion }
		plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.025 }
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		years = 333

		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.10 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.20 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.30 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.40 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.50 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.60 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.70 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.80 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 0.90 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power_contribution = { plotter = ROOT power = 1.5 } }

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = { has_plot = plot_make_voodoo_doll plot_power = 1.0 }
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = { has_plot = plot_make_voodoo_doll plot_power = 1.5 }
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = { has_plot = plot_make_voodoo_doll plot_power = 2.0 }
		}
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 plot_target_char = { trait = creature_halfling } }
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 plot_target_char = { trait = creature_dwarf } }
		modifier = { factor = 1.75 plot_target_char = { trait = creature_elf } }
		modifier = { factor = 2.75 plot_target_char = { trait = vampire_strigoi_visible } }
		modifier = { factor = 5 plot_target_char = { trait = vampire_necrarch_visible } }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp200 # The conspirators provide support
		trigger = { plot_power = 1.0 }
		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = vamp.212 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		plot_target_char = { character_event = { id = vamp.205 days = 30 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBvamp200 # Provide bribery
		trigger = { NOT = { plot_power = 0.5 } NOT = { learning = 10 } }
		scaled_wealth = -1.0

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = vamp.212 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		plot_target_char = { character_event = { id = vamp.205 days = 30 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBvamp200 # Provide bribery
		trigger = { plot_power = 0.5 NOT = { plot_power = 1.0 } NOT = { learning = 10 } }
		scaled_wealth = -0.5

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = vamp.212 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		plot_target_char = { character_event = { id = vamp.205 days = 30 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCvamp200 # Offer tactical help
		tooltip_info = learning
		trigger = { plot_power = 0.5 learning = 10 }

		hidden_tooltip = { any_plot_backer = { character_event = { id = vamp.212 days = 1 } } }

		set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		plot_target_char = { character_event = { id = vamp.205 days = 30 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDvamp200 # I cannot afford this
		trigger = { ai = no NOT = { learning = 10 } NOT = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
	}
}

# vamp.201 The voodoo doll: a backer's chance

	prisoner = no

				age = 16
				prisoner = no
				NOT = { trait = in_hiding }
				NOT = { trait = questing }

				NOT = { trait = monster_hunting }
			}

		}
	}

		years = 200

				has_plot = plot_make_voodoo_doll

		}

				has_plot = plot_make_voodoo_doll
				ROOT = { plot_power_contribution = { plotter = PREV power = 0.30 } }
			}
		}

			factor = 0.6
			any_backed_character = {
				has_plot = plot_make_voodoo_doll

		}

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.33
			any_backed_character = { has_plot = plot_make_voodoo_doll plot_power = 1.0 }
		}

		}

		name = EVTOPTAvamp200 # The plot pays
		trigger = { any_backed_character = { has_plot = plot_make_voodoo_doll plot_power = 1.0 } }
		random_backed_character = {
			limit = { has_plot = plot_make_voodoo_doll plot_power = 1.0 }
			character_event = { id = vamp.210 days = 3 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp201 # Ask the Plotter for funds
		trigger = {
			NOT = { learning = 10 }
			NOT = { any_backed_character = { has_plot = plot_make_voodoo_doll plot_power = 1.0 } }
		}
		random_backed_character = {
			limit = { has_plot = plot_make_voodoo_doll NOT = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
			character_event = { id = vamp.202 days = 3 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBvamp201 # Provide funds
		trigger = {
			NOT = { learning = 10 }
			NOT = { any_backed_character = { has_plot = plot_make_voodoo_doll plot_power = 1.0 } }
		}
		scaled_wealth = -0.5
		random_backed_character = {
			limit = { has_plot = plot_make_voodoo_doll NOT = { plot_power = 1.0 } }
			character_event = { id = vamp.210 days = 3 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCvamp200 # Provide tactical help.
		tooltip_info = learning
		trigger = {
			learning = 10
			any_backed_character = { has_plot = plot_make_voodoo_doll plot_power = 0.5 }
		}

		random_backed_character = {
			limit = { has_plot = plot_make_voodoo_doll plot_power = 0.5 }
			character_event = { id = vamp.210 days = 3 }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDvamp200 # I cannot afford this
		trigger = {
			ai = no
			NOT = { learning = 10 }
			NOT = { any_backed_character = { has_plot = plot_make_voodoo_doll plot_power = 1.0 } }
		}
	}
}

# vamp.205 A shadow in the chamber
# The thief comes for something of the target's. The paranoid, the deceitful
# and those who know the Shadowmen are harder to fool. Theft and discovery
# are rolled separately (vamp.206 to vamp.209, plotter notes vamp.213 to
# vamp.216).
# Scope: ROOT is the target, FROM the plotter or backer, FROMFROM the plotter.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.205
	desc = EVTDESCvamp205
	picture = GFX_evt_feast
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		prisoner = no

		# Is the plot still in motion?
		OR = {
			FROM = { has_plot = plot_make_voodoo_doll plot_target_char = { character = ROOT } }
			FROMFROM = { has_plot = plot_make_voodoo_doll plot_target_char = { character = ROOT } }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		save_event_target_as = target_victim
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp205 # Does not notice weird behavior
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					AND = { trait = paranoid random = 33 }
					AND = { trait = deceitful random = 50 }
					AND = {
						religion = shadowmen #Knows the danger
						random = 60
					}
					AND = {
						religion = akghan #Knows the danger
						random = 60
					}
					AND = { trait = trusting random = 90 }
					AND = {
						NOT = { trait = paranoid }
						NOT = { trait = trusting }
						NOT = { trait = deceitful }
						NOT = { religion = shadowmen }
						NOT = { religion = akghan }
						random = 75
					}
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				25 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0 FROM = { has_character_flag = using_shadow_plot } }
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.213 days = 1 } }
						if = {
							limit = { FROMFROM = { NOT = { character = FROM } } }
							FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.213 days = 1 } }
						}
					}
					character_event = { id = vamp.206 days = 1 } # Object stolen, he notices
				}
				75 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.214 days = 1 } }
						if = {
							limit = { FROMFROM = { NOT = { character = FROM } } }
							FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.214 days = 1 } }
						}
					}
					character_event = { id = vamp.207 days = 1 } # Object stolen, he doesn't notices
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBvamp205 # Notices weird movement
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = { trait = paranoid random = 33 }
				AND = { trait = deceitful random = 50 }
				AND = {
					religion = shadowmen #Knows the danger
					random = 60
				}
				AND = {
					religion = akghan #Knows the danger
					random = 60
				}
				AND = { trait = trusting random = 90 }
				AND = {
					NOT = { trait = paranoid }
					NOT = { trait = trusting }
					NOT = { trait = deceitful }
					NOT = { religion = shadowmen }
					NOT = { religion = akghan }
					random = 75
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0 FROM = { has_character_flag = using_shadow_plot } }
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.215 days = 1 } }
						if = {
							limit = { FROMFROM = { NOT = { character = FROM } } }
							FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.215 days = 1 } }
						}
					}
					character_event = { id = vamp.208 days = 1 } # Lives, plotter discovered
				}
				50 = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.216 days = 1 } }
						if = {
							limit = { FROMFROM = { NOT = { character = FROM } } }
							FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.216 days = 1 } }
						}
					}
					character_event = { id = vamp.209 days = 1 } # Lives, plotter unknown
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# vamp.222 Choose the curse
# Curse the target and their lands (vamp.225, Mahtmasi or great sorcerers),
# their lands only (vamp.240) or their person (vamp.255).
# Scope: ROOT is the plotter, FROM the target.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.222
	desc = EVTDESCvamp222
	picture = GFX_evt_voodoo_shaman
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	#Curse him and all his lands (tier 4)
	#Curse his lands (tier 2)
	#Curse him (tier 1)
	immediate = {
		clr_character_flag = voodoo_success_flag
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp222
		trigger = {
			AND = {
				FROM = { higher_real_tier_than = BARON }
				OR = { trait = vampire_mahtmasi_visible has_character_modifier = magic_power_4 }
			}
		}
		FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.225 days = 2 random = 5 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBvamp222
		trigger = {
			AND = {
				FROM = { higher_real_tier_than = BARON }
				OR = {
					trait = vampire_mahtmasi_visible
					has_character_modifier = magic_power_2
					has_character_modifier = magic_power_3
					has_character_modifier = magic_power_4
				}
			}
		}
		FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.240 days = 2 random = 5 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCvamp222
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				trait = vampire_mahtmasi_visible
				has_character_modifier = magic_power_2
				has_character_modifier = magic_power_3
				has_character_modifier = magic_power_4
				has_character_modifier = magic_power_1
			}
		}
		FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.255 days = 2 random = 5 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
	option = { #Changed my mind
		name = EVTOPTDvamp222
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

# vamp.225 A curse on body and land
# The target falls ill and the capital is cursed. Vampires and mages may try
# to break it (vamp.226), or the plotter goes on (vamp.227).
# Scope: ROOT is the target, FROM the plotter.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.225
	desc = EVTDESCvamp225
	picture = GFX_evt_bad_news
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		add_symptom_effect = yes
		nurgle_blessing_check_effect = yes
		capital_scope = {
			add_province_modifier = { name = cursed_lands duration = 465 stacking = yes }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp225
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				is_vampire_trigger = yes
				has_character_modifier = magic_power_2
				has_character_modifier = magic_power_3
				has_character_modifier = magic_power_4
				has_character_modifier = magic_power_1
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				75 = {
					FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.226 } } #broke enchantment
				}
				25 = {
					FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.227 } } #failed to break enchantment
					modifier = { factor = 0.95 trait = magic_potential_2 }
					modifier = { factor = 0.85 trait = magic_potential_3 }
					modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = magic_potential_4 }
					modifier = { factor = 0.75 is_vampire_trigger = yes }
					modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = vampire_necrarch_visible }
					modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = lore_heavens trait = vampire_jade_visible }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_character_modifier = magic_power_1 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.15 has_character_modifier = magic_power_2 }
					modifier = { factor = 0.85 has_character_modifier = magic_power_3 }
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 has_character_modifier = magic_power_4 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = hedge_wizard }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBvamp225
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.227 } } }
	}
}

# vamp.232 Darkness II
# Depression, wounds and an angry court.
# Scope: ROOT is the target, FROM the plotter.

	id = vamp.232
	desc = EVTDESCvamp232
	picture = GFX_evt_bad_news
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp232
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = depressed } } add_trait = depressed }
		random_list = {
			20 = { add_trait = maimed }
			10 = { add_trait = blinded }
			70 = { }
		}
		any_courtier = { opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_cursed months = 24 } }
		any_realm_province = {
			limit = { owner = { character = ROOT } NOT = { culture = nehekharan } }
			add_province_modifier = { name = cursed_lands duration = 200 stacking = yes }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.233 days = 5 random = 14 } } }
	}

# vamp.234 Darkness III
# Family members die and the vassals turn angry. Repeats while the plotter
# keeps it going (vamp.233).
# Scope: ROOT is the target, FROM the plotter.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.234
	desc = EVTDESCvamp234
	picture = GFX_evt_bad_news
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp234
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = depressed } } add_trait = depressed }
		random_list = {
			20 = { add_trait = incapable }
			10 = { vamp_curse_kill_effect = yes }
			70 = { }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { any_child = { is_ruler = no is_invincible_trigger = no NOT = { age = 20 } } }
			random_child = {
				limit = { is_ruler = no is_invincible_trigger = no NOT = { age = 20 } }
				death = { death_reason = death_murder_unknown }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_spouse = { is_ruler = no is_invincible_trigger = no is_vampire_trigger = no }
			}
			random_spouse = {
				limit = { is_ruler = no is_invincible_trigger = no is_vampire_trigger = no }
				death = { death_reason = death_murder_unknown }
			}
		}
		any_vassal = {
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_cursed months = 24 }
			capital_scope = {
				add_province_modifier = { name = cursed_lands duration = 40 stacking = yes }
			}
		}
		any_realm_province = {
			limit = { owner = { character = ROOT } NOT = { culture = nehekharan } }
			add_province_modifier = { name = cursed_lands duration = 80 stacking = yes }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.233 days = 5 random = 14 } } }
	}
}

			70 = { }
		}

			limit = { any_child = { is_ruler = no is_invincible_trigger = no NOT = { age = 20 } } }

				limit = { is_ruler = no is_invincible_trigger = no NOT = { age = 20 } }
				death = { death_reason = death_murder_unknown }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_spouse = { is_ruler = no is_invincible_trigger = no is_vampire_trigger = no }
			}
			random_spouse = {
				limit = { is_ruler = no is_invincible_trigger = no is_vampire_trigger = no }
				death = { death_reason = death_murder_unknown }
			}
		}
		any_vassal = {
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_cursed months = 1 }
			capital_scope = {
				add_province_modifier = { name = locust_plague duration = 40 stacking = yes }
			}
		}
		any_realm_province = {

# vamp.240 A curse on the land
# The capital is cursed; vampires and mages may try to break it (vamp.226),
# or the plotter goes on (vamp.241).
# Scope: ROOT is the target, FROM the plotter.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.240
	desc = EVTDESCvamp240
	picture = GFX_evt_bad_news
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		capital_scope = {
			add_province_modifier = { name = cursed_lands duration = 465 stacking = yes }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp240
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				is_vampire_trigger = yes
				has_character_modifier = magic_power_2
				has_character_modifier = magic_power_3
				has_character_modifier = magic_power_4
				has_character_modifier = magic_power_1
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				75 = {
					FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.226 } } #broke enchantment
				}
				25 = {
					FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.241 } } #failed to break enchantment
					modifier = { factor = 0.95 trait = magic_potential_2 }
					modifier = { factor = 0.85 trait = magic_potential_3 }
					modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = magic_potential_4 }
					modifier = { factor = 0.75 is_vampire_trigger = yes }
					modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = vampire_necrarch_visible }
					modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = lore_heavens trait = vampire_jade_visible }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_character_modifier = magic_power_1 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.15 has_character_modifier = magic_power_2 }
					modifier = { factor = 0.85 has_character_modifier = magic_power_3 }
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 has_character_modifier = magic_power_4 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = hedge_wizard }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBvamp240
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.241 } } }
	}
}

# vamp.256 Work the doll
# Pin it (vamp.260), carve its eyes (vamp.262), squeeze it (vamp.264) or try
# something else (vamp.257).

	id = vamp.256
	desc = EVTDESCvamp256
	picture = GFX_evt_voodoo_shaman
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { #Pin the doll
		name = EVTOPTAvamp256
		FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.260 days = 5 random = 14 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 25 }
	}
	option = { #Carve the doll's eyes
		name = EVTOPTBvamp256
		trigger = { FROM = { NOT = { trait = blinded } } }
		FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.262 days = 5 random = 14 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 25 }
	}
	option = { #Squash the doll
		name = EVTOPTCvamp256
		FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.264 days = 5 random = 14 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 25 }
	}
	option = { #More
		name = EVTOPTDvamp256
		FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.257 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 25 }
	}
}

# vamp.258 Work the doll II
# Cut it (vamp.266), boil its head (vamp.268), bind its heart (vamp.270) or go
# back (vamp.259).
# Scope: ROOT is the plotter, FROM the target.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.258
	desc = EVTDESCvamp258
	picture = GFX_evt_voodoo_shaman
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { #Cut the doll's tiny bits
		name = EVTOPTAvamp258
		trigger = { FROM = { NOR = { trait = eunuch trait = infertile } } }
		FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.266 days = 5 random = 14 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 25 }
	}
	option = { #Boil the doll's head
		name = EVTOPTBvamp258
		FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.268 days = 5 random = 14 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 25 }
	}
	option = { #Bind the doll's heart
		name = EVTOPTCvamp258
		trigger = {
			FROM = { OR = { has_lover = yes any_friend = { is_alive = yes } is_married = yes } }
		}
		FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.270 days = 5 random = 14 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 25 }
	}
	option = { #Back to previous
		name = EVTOPTDvamp258
		FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.259 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 25 }
	}
}

# vamp.259 Back

# vamp.260 Pinned
# Each pin is worse: wounded, then maimed, then incapable, then death.
# Scope: ROOT is the target, FROM the plotter.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.260
	desc = EVTDESCvamp260
	picture = GFX_evt_bad_news
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp260
		if = { limit = { trait = incapable } vamp_curse_kill_effect = yes }
		else_if = { limit = { trait = maimed } add_trait = incapable }
		else_if = { limit = { trait = wounded } add_trait = maimed }
		else = { add_trait = wounded }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.261 days = 5 random = 14 } } }
	}
}

# vamp.268 Head boiled
# Madness, and sometimes infirmity and lost learning.
# Scope: ROOT is the target, FROM the plotter.

	id = vamp.268
	desc = EVTDESCvamp268
	picture = GFX_evt_lunatic
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp268
		random = {
			chance = 20
			if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = infirm } } add_trait = infirm }
			change_learning = -1
		}
		change_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = 7 }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			character_event = { id = mutate.5 }
			FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.269 days = 5 random = 14 } }
		}
	}
}

# vamp.270 Heart bound
# A lover, friend or spouse turns against the target.
# Scope: ROOT is the target, FROM the plotter.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.270
	desc = EVTDESCvamp270
	picture = GFX_evt_lunatic
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp270
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.271 days = 5 random = 14 } } }
		if = { limit = { has_lover = yes } random_lover = { remove_lover = ROOT add_rival = ROOT } }
		else_if = {
			limit = { any_friend = { is_alive = yes } }
			random_friend = { remove_friend = ROOT add_rival = ROOT }
		}
		else_if = { limit = { is_married = yes } random_spouse = { add_rival = ROOT } }
	}
}

###############################################################################
# The Blood Quest
# A Blood Dragon sets out with the vampires of his court and vassals to
# hunt worthy foes across the world, as Abhorash taught (decision
# start_blood_quest). The leader picks a direction now and then; on the way
# the band meets greenskins, ogres, beastmen, vampire hunters and champions
# who challenge him. Each fight is decided by the host's strength
# (vamp_bquest_strength_effect) and the knights who ride in. The quest runs
# until the leader stops it (stop_blood_questing), dies or is captured.
# Shared code: vamp_bquest_* in wh_vamp_effects.txt and wh_vamp_triggers.txt.
###############################################################################

# vamp.300 The quest begins
# Scope: ROOT is the leader. Fired by start_blood_quest.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.300
	title = BLOOD_QUEST_TITLE
	desc = EVTDESCvamp300
	picture = GFX_evt_vampire_knight
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp300
		vamp_bquest_region_here_effect = yes
		vamp_bquest_region_tooltip_effect = yes
		hidden_effect = { character_event = { id = vamp.308 days = 4 } }
	}
}

# vamp.301 The call to the quest
# A vampire of the leader's court or realm is asked to ride along.
# Scope: ROOT is the vampire; FROM the leader. Fired by start_blood_quest.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.301
	title = BLOOD_QUEST_TITLE
	desc = EVTDESCvamp301
	picture = GFX_evt_vampire_knight
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Ride
		name = EVTOPTAvamp301
		add_trait = blood_questing
		opinion = { modifier = opinion_blood_questing who = FROM }
		hidden_effect = {
			add_character_modifier = { name = blood_quest_startup duration = 150 hidden = yes }
			FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.302 } }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 70
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = brave }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = wroth }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = craven }
		}
	}

	option = { # Stay
		name = EVTOPTBvamp301
		prestige = -50
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 30
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 NOT = { opinion = { who = FROM value = -20 } } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 NOT = { opinion = { who = FROM value = -40 } } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 NOT = { opinion = { who = FROM value = -60 } } }
			modifier = { factor = 2 NOT = { opinion = { who = FROM value = -80 } } }
		}
	}
}

# vamp.302 A knight joins
# Scope: ROOT is the leader; FROM the knight.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.302
	desc = EVTDESCvamp302
	picture = GFX_evt_vampire_knight
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	notification = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp302
		show_portrait = FROM
	}
}

# vamp.303 Home from the quest
# Scope: ROOT is the vampire who ends the quest. Fired by stop_blood_questing.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.303
	title = BLOOD_QUEST_TITLE
	desc = EVTDESCvamp303
	picture = GFX_evt_vampire_knight
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp303
	}
}

# vamp.304 A knight rides home
# A ruler of the band gives up the quest; the leader is told.
# Scope: ROOT is the leader; FROM the knight. Fired by stop_blood_questing.
letter_event = {
	id = vamp.304
	desc = EVTDESCvamp304
	border = GFX_event_letter_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp304
		FROM = { remove_opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_blood_questing } }
	}
}

# vamp.305 The band disbands
# The leader ended the quest, fell, or was captured; the knights ride home.
# Scope: ROOT is a knight; FROM the leader. Fired by stop_blood_questing and
# vamp.306.
letter_event = {
	id = vamp.305
	desc = { trigger = { FROM = { is_alive = no } } text = EVTDESCvamp305b }
	desc = { trigger = { FROM = { is_alive = yes prisoner = yes } } text = EVTDESCvamp305c }
	desc = { trigger = { FROM = { is_alive = yes prisoner = no } } text = EVTDESCvamp305 }
	border = GFX_event_letter_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp305
		vamp_bquest_leave_effect = yes
		remove_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_blood_questing }
	}
}

# vamp.306 The quest ends with its leader
# A questing vampire dies or is captured. A leader's band disbands (vamp.305).
# Scope: ROOT is the vampire. Fired by on_death and
# on_become_imprisoned_any_reason.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.306
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes
	has_character_flag = blood_quest_leader

	immediate = {
		any_realm_character = {
			limit = { vamp_bquest_band_trigger = yes }
			letter_event = { id = vamp.305 }
		}
		vamp_bquest_leave_effect = yes
	}
}

# vamp.307 A knight is captured
# A questing knight who is taken prisoner leaves the band.
# Scope: ROOT is the knight. Fired by on_become_imprisoned_any_reason.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.307
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = blood_questing
		NOT = { has_character_flag = blood_quest_leader }
	}

	immediate = {
		vamp_bquest_leave_effect = yes
	}
}

# vamp.308 Stay or ride on
# Scope: ROOT is the leader. Fired by vamp.300 and vamp_bquest_next_effect.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.308
	title = BLOOD_QUEST_TITLE
	desc = EVTDESCvamp308
	picture = GFX_evt_vampire_knight
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes
	has_character_flag = blood_quest_leader

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_character_flag = ocean_questing }
	}

	immediate = {
		vamp_bquest_clear_fight_effect = yes
	}

	option = { # Stay
		name = EVTOPTAvamp308
		vamp_bquest_region_tooltip_effect = yes
		hidden_effect = { character_event = { id = vamp.311 days = 10 random = 10 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 60 }
	}

	option = { # Ride on
		name = EVTOPTBvamp308
		hidden_effect = { character_event = { id = vamp.309 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 40 }
	}
}

# vamp.309 Which way?
# North, east, west or south of where the quest is now. From some lands the
# way leads over the Great Ocean.
# Scope: ROOT is the leader. Fired by vamp.308 and vamp.352.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.309
	title = BLOOD_QUEST_TITLE
	desc = EVTDESCvamp309
	picture = GFX_evt_vampire_knight
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes
	has_character_flag = blood_quest_leader

	option = { # North
		name = EVTOPTAvamp309
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = empire_questing }
			custom_tooltip = { text = travelling_norsca }
			hidden_effect = {
				vamp_bquest_clear_region_effect = yes
				set_character_flag = norsca_questing
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = border_princes_questing }
			custom_tooltip = { text = travelling_empire }
			hidden_effect = {
				vamp_bquest_clear_region_effect = yes
				set_character_flag = empire_questing
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = kislev_questing }
			custom_tooltip = { text = travelling_chaos_wastes }
			hidden_effect = {
				vamp_bquest_clear_region_effect = yes
				set_character_flag = chaos_wastes_questing
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = bretonnia_questing }
			custom_tooltip = { text = travelling_empire }
			hidden_effect = {
				vamp_bquest_clear_region_effect = yes
				set_character_flag = empire_questing
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = estilea_questing }

			hidden_effect = {
				vamp_bquest_clear_region_effect = yes
				set_character_flag = bretonnia_questing
			}

# vamp.311 What the road brings
# Picks the next encounter. Greenskins are thick in the Badlands, Border
# Princes, mountains and Darklands; ogres in the mountains and the east;
# beastmen in the forests of the Empire, Bretonnia and Athel Loren; vampire
# hunters in the Old World. At sea there is only the crossing (vamp.352).
# Scope: ROOT is the leader. Fired by vamp.308 and vamp_bquest_next_effect.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.311
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes
	has_character_flag = blood_quest_leader

	immediate = {
		vamp_bquest_clear_fight_effect = yes
		if = { limit = { has_character_flag = ocean_questing } character_event = { id = vamp.352 } }
		else = {
			random_list = {
				5 = { # Greenskins
					modifier = {
						factor = 2
						OR = {
							has_character_flag = badlands_questing
							has_character_flag = border_princes_questing
							has_character_flag = mountains_questing
							has_character_flag = darklands_questing
						}
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.2
						OR = {
							has_character_flag = ulthuan_questing
							has_character_flag = lustria_questing
							has_character_flag = naggaroth_questing
						}
					}
					character_event = { id = vamp.320 }
				}
				3 = { # Ogres
					modifier = {
						factor = 2
						OR = {
							has_character_flag = mountains_questing
							has_character_flag = darklands_questing
						}
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.2
						OR = {
							has_character_flag = ulthuan_questing
							has_character_flag = lustria_questing
							has_character_flag = naggaroth_questing
						}
					}
					character_event = { id = vamp.330 }
				}
				3 = { # Beastmen
					modifier = {
						factor = 2
						OR = {
							has_character_flag = empire_questing
							has_character_flag = bretonnia_questing
							has_character_flag = athel_loren_questing
							has_character_flag = kislev_questing
						}
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.1
						OR = {
							has_character_flag = araby_questing
							has_character_flag = nehekhara_questing
							has_character_flag = ulthuan_questing
						}
					}
					character_event = { id = vamp.340 }
				}
				2 = { # Vampire hunters
					modifier = {
						factor = 2
						OR = {
							has_character_flag = empire_questing
							has_character_flag = bretonnia_questing
							has_character_flag = estilea_questing
							has_character_flag = border_princes_questing
							has_character_flag = kislev_questing
						}
					}
					character_event = { id = vamp.345 }
				}
				3 = { character_event = { id = vamp.350 } } # A champion's challenge
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################################
# The Blood Quest: the knights in a fight
# Shared by every encounter: the leader calls his knights (vamp.312), they
# ride in beside him (vamp.313) and share the victory (vamp.314). The
# encounter and the leader's plan are flags on the leader (bquest_<foe>,
# bquest_tac_<plan>).
###############################################################################

# vamp.312 The call to arms
# Scope: ROOT is a knight; FROM the leader.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.312
	desc = { trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = bquest_orcs } } text = EVTDESCvamp312 }
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				has_character_flag = bquest_ogres
				NOT = { has_character_flag = bquest_tac_ambush }
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESCvamp312b
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			FROM = { has_character_flag = bquest_ogres has_character_flag = bquest_tac_ambush }
		}
		text = EVTDESCvamp312c
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				has_character_flag = bquest_beastmen
				NOT = { has_character_flag = bquest_tac_fallback }
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESCvamp312d
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			FROM = { has_character_flag = bquest_beastmen has_character_flag = bquest_tac_fallback }
		}
		text = EVTDESCvamp312e
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = bquest_adventurers } }
		text = EVTDESCvamp312f
	}
	picture = { trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = bquest_orcs } } picture = GFX_evt_orcs }
	picture = {
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = bquest_adventurers } }
		picture = GFX_evt_witch_hunters
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = bquest_ogres } }
		picture = GFX_evt_ogre_raiders
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = bquest_beastmen } }
		picture = GFX_evt_beastmen
	}
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Ride in
		name = EVTOPTAvamp312
		set_character_flag = bquest_joined
		hidden_effect = { FROM = { change_variable = { which = blood_host_strength value = 3 } } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 75
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = brave }
			modifier = { factor = 0.2 trait = incapable }
		}
	}

	option = { # Hold back
		name = EVTOPTBvamp312
		FROM = { opinion = { modifier = insulted who = ROOT } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 25
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = craven }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = wounded }
		}
	}
}

# vamp.313 Into the fray
# The knight fights beside the leader, by the leader's plan.
# Scope: ROOT is a knight; FROM the leader.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.313
	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = bquest_tac_sky } has_artifact = nightmare_mount }
		text = EVTDESCvamp313
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			FROM = { has_character_flag = bquest_tac_sky }
			NOT = { has_artifact = nightmare_mount }
		}
		text = EVTDESCvamp313b
	}
	desc = { trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = bquest_tac_lure } } text = EVTDESCvamp313c }
	desc = { trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = bquest_tac_flank } } text = EVTDESCvamp313d }
	desc = {
		trigger = {

# vamp.315 Defeat
# The foe breaks the host. The knights drag the leader from the field, or he
# falls (vamp_bquest_defeat_effect). The knights who rode in go home with
# nothing.
# Scope: ROOT is the leader.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.315
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = bquest_orcs } text = EVTDESCvamp315 }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = bquest_ogres } text = EVTDESCvamp315b }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = bquest_beastmen } text = EVTDESCvamp315c }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = bquest_adventurers } text = EVTDESCvamp315d }
	picture = { trigger = { has_character_flag = bquest_orcs } picture = GFX_evt_orcs_charging }
	picture = { trigger = { has_character_flag = bquest_ogres } picture = GFX_evt_ogre_raiders }
	picture = { trigger = { has_character_flag = bquest_beastmen } picture = GFX_evt_beastmen }
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = bquest_adventurers }
		picture = GFX_evt_adventurers
	}
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp315
		prestige = -100
		hidden_effect = {
			any_realm_character = {
				limit = { vamp_bquest_fighter_trigger = yes }
				clr_character_flag = bquest_joined
			}
		}
		vamp_bquest_next_effect = yes # Before the roll: a fallen leader ends the quest
		vamp_bquest_defeat_effect = yes
	}
}

# vamp.320 Greenskins sighted
# Scope: ROOT is the leader. Fired by vamp.311.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.320
	title = BLOOD_QUEST_TITLE
	desc = EVTDESCvamp320
	picture = GFX_evt_orcs
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes
	has_character_flag = blood_quest_leader

	option = { # Fight
		name = EVTOPTAvamp320
		hidden_effect = {
			vamp_bquest_strength_effect = yes
			set_character_flag = bquest_orcs
			any_realm_character = {
				limit = { vamp_bquest_band_trigger = yes }
				character_event = { id = vamp.312 }
			}
			character_event = { id = vamp.321 days = 2 }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 85 }
	}

	option = { # Ride around
		name = EVTOPTBvamp320
		prestige = -25
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = brave } } random = { chance = 5 add_trait = craven } }
		vamp_bquest_next_effect = yes
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 15
			modifier = { factor = 3 trait = craven }
			modifier = { factor = 3 trait = wounded }
		}
	}
}

# vamp.321 The battle plan
# The plan adds to the host's strength: from the sky on nightmares (best), a
# lure for a clever leader, a flank attack for a skilled one, or a plain
# charge (better for the brave).
# Scope: ROOT is the leader.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.321
	title = BLOOD_QUEST_TITLE
	desc = EVTDESCvamp321
	picture = GFX_evt_orcs
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes
	has_character_flag = blood_quest_leader

	option = { # From the sky
		name = EVTOPTAvamp321
		trigger = { has_artifact = nightmare_mount }
		set_character_flag = bquest_tac_sky
		hidden_effect = {
			change_variable = { which = blood_host_strength value = 4 }
			any_realm_character = {
				limit = { vamp_bquest_fighter_trigger = yes }
				character_event = { id = vamp.313 }
			}
			character_event = { id = vamp.322 days = 1 }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
	}

	option = { # Lure them down
		name = EVTOPTBvamp321
		trigger = { OR = { trait = quick trait = genius trait = shrewd trait = strategist } }
		set_character_flag = bquest_tac_lure
		hidden_effect = {
			change_variable = { which = blood_host_strength value = 3 }
			any_realm_character = {
				limit = { vamp_bquest_fighter_trigger = yes }
				character_event = { id = vamp.313 }
			}
			character_event = { id = vamp.322 days = 1 }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 40 }
	}

	option = { # Flank them
		name = EVTOPTCvamp321
		set_character_flag = bquest_tac_flank
		hidden_effect = {
			change_variable = { which = blood_host_strength value = 2 }
			if = {
				limit = { martial = 15 }
				change_variable = { which = blood_host_strength value = 1 }
			}
			any_realm_character = {
				limit = { vamp_bquest_fighter_trigger = yes }
				character_event = { id = vamp.313 }
			}
			character_event = { id = vamp.322 days = 1 }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 30 modifier = { factor = 1.5 martial = 15 } }
	}

	option = { # Charge
		name = EVTOPTDvamp321
		set_character_flag = bquest_tac_charge
		hidden_effect = {
			change_variable = { which = blood_host_strength value = 1 }
			if = {
				limit = { OR = { trait = brave trait = wroth } }
				change_variable = { which = blood_host_strength value = 2 }
			}
			any_realm_character = {
				limit = { vamp_bquest_fighter_trigger = yes }
				character_event = { id = vamp.313 }
			}
			character_event = { id = vamp.322 days = 1 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 20
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = brave }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = wroth }
		}
	}
}

# vamp.323 The greenskins break
# Scope: ROOT is the leader.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.323
	title = BLOOD_QUEST_TITLE
	desc = EVTDESCvamp323
	picture = GFX_evt_greenskindeath
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes
	has_character_flag = blood_quest_leader

	immediate = {
		vamp_bquest_fighters_win_effect = yes
	}

	option = { # Pursue and destroy
		name = EVTOPTAvamp323
		prestige = 25
		vamp_bquest_trophy_effect = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				75 = { set_character_flag = bquest_pursuit_destroyed }
				25 = { set_character_flag = bquest_pursuit_lost }
			}
			character_event = { id = vamp.325 days = 1 }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 40 modifier = { factor = 2 trait = wroth } }
	}

	option = { # Pursue and capture
		name = EVTOPTBvamp323
		prestige = 25
		vamp_bquest_trophy_effect = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				60 = { set_character_flag = bquest_pursuit_captured }
				40 = { set_character_flag = bquest_pursuit_lost }
			}
			character_event = { id = vamp.325 days = 1 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 40
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = greedy }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = cruel }
		}
	}

	option = { # Let them flee
		name = EVTOPTCvamp323
		prestige = 25
		vamp_bquest_trophy_effect = yes
		vamp_bquest_next_effect = yes
		ai_chance = { factor = 20 }
	}
}

# vamp.325 The pursuit

	id = vamp.325
	title = BLOOD_QUEST_TITLE

	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = bquest_pursuit_captured } text = EVTDESCvamp325b }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = bquest_pursuit_lost } text = EVTDESCvamp325c }
	picture = GFX_evt_greenskindeath
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes
	has_character_flag = blood_quest_leader

	option = {
		name = { trigger = { has_character_flag = bquest_pursuit_destroyed } text = EVTOPTAvamp325 }
		name = { trigger = { has_character_flag = bquest_pursuit_captured } text = EVTOPTAvamp325b }
		name = { trigger = { has_character_flag = bquest_pursuit_lost } text = EVTOPTAvamp325c }
		if = { limit = { has_character_flag = bquest_pursuit_destroyed } prestige = 50 }
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = bquest_pursuit_captured }
			scaled_wealth = { value = 0.5 min = 25 max = 250 }
		}
		vamp_bquest_next_effect = yes
	}
}

# vamp.340 Beastmen in the mist
# The host sheltered from the sun in the wrong forest. There is no riding by.
# Falling back to turn and charge is better, more so for a good organizer of
# men or a cavalry leader.
# Scope: ROOT is the leader. Fired by vamp.311.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.340
	title = BLOOD_QUEST_TITLE
	desc = EVTDESCvamp340
	picture = GFX_evt_beastmen_mist
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes
	has_character_flag = blood_quest_leader

	option = { # Charge
		name = EVTOPTAvamp340
		set_character_flag = bquest_tac_charge
		hidden_effect = {
			vamp_bquest_strength_effect = yes
			change_variable = { which = blood_host_strength value = 1 }
			if = {
				limit = { OR = { trait = brave trait = wroth } }
				change_variable = { which = blood_host_strength value = 2 }
			}
			set_character_flag = bquest_beastmen
			any_realm_character = {
				limit = { vamp_bquest_band_trigger = yes }
				character_event = { id = vamp.312 }
			}
			character_event = { id = vamp.341 days = 1 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = brave }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = wroth }
		}
	}

	option = { # Fall back
		name = EVTOPTBvamp340
		set_character_flag = bquest_tac_fallback
		hidden_effect = {
			vamp_bquest_strength_effect = yes
			change_variable = { which = blood_host_strength value = 2 }
			if = {
				limit = { OR = { trait = organizer trait = cavalry_leader trait = strategist } }
				change_variable = { which = blood_host_strength value = 3 }
			}
			set_character_flag = bquest_beastmen
			any_realm_character = {
				limit = { vamp_bquest_band_trigger = yes }
				character_event = { id = vamp.312 }
			}
			character_event = { id = vamp.341 days = 1 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = organizer }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = cavalry_leader }
		}
	}
}

	}
}

		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				has_character_flag = bretonnia_questing
				has_character_flag = empire_questing
				has_character_flag = kislev_questing
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESCvamp350d
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = bretonnia_questing }
		picture = GFX_evt_bretonnian_knight_mounted
	}

		picture = GFX_evt_joust
	}

						check_variable = { which = blood_host_strength value = 10 }
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.25
						check_variable = { which = blood_host_strength value = 20 }
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.25
						check_variable = { which = blood_host_strength value = 30 }
					}
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = duelist }
					set_character_flag = bquest_duel_won
				}
				50 = {
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = wounded }
					modifier = { factor = 2 trait = maimed }
					set_character_flag = bquest_duel_lost
				}
			}
			character_event = { id = vamp.351 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 85
			modifier = { factor = 0.2 trait = craven }
			modifier = { factor = 0.3 trait = maimed }
		}
	}

	option = { # Refuse
		name = EVTOPTBvamp350
		prestige = -75
		vamp_bquest_next_effect = yes
		ai_chance = { factor = 15 }
	}
}

# vamp.351 The duel
# Scope: ROOT is the leader.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.351
	title = BLOOD_QUEST_TITLE
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = bquest_duel_won } text = EVTDESCvamp351 }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = bquest_duel_lost } text = EVTDESCvamp351b }
	picture = GFX_evt_joust
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes
	has_character_flag = blood_quest_leader

	option = {
		name = { trigger = { has_character_flag = bquest_duel_won } text = EVTOPTAvamp351 }
		name = { trigger = { has_character_flag = bquest_duel_lost } text = EVTOPTAvamp351b }
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = bquest_duel_won }
			prestige = 150
			vamp_bquest_trophy_effect = yes
			vamp_bquest_next_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			prestige = -50
			vamp_bquest_next_effect = yes # Before the roll: a fallen leader ends the quest
			random_list = {
				60 = { modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = wounded } add_trait_wounded_effect = yes }
				35 = { add_trait = maimed }
				5 = {
					modifier = { factor = 2 trait = maimed }
					if = { limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes } antideath_effect = yes }
					else = { death = { death_reason = death_duel } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################################
# The Lahmian Sisterhood
# The heirs of Neferata rule from the shadows. The Lahmian queen sends a
# seductress (a daughter, an elite agent or herself) to a mortal ruler's court
# (decisions send_a_seductress and seduce_target_personally). She plays the
# damsel in distress, hosts a ball, or poisons his wife and comforts the
# widower. Once he is hers, she asks him every year for gold or troops for her
# queen (vamp.430). The queen can also call a mortal lover to her court
# (bring_lover_home), send a daughter to embrace new sisters
# (send_on_recruitment_mission) and plant hidden houses in mortal lands that
# send her money and draw witch hunters (establish_lahmian_presence).
# Shared code: vamp_lahmia_* in wh_vamp_effects.txt and wh_vamp_triggers.txt.
###############################################################################

# vamp.400 Life eternal
# The Lahmian queen's introduction.
# Scope: ROOT is the player. Fired by on_startup.
long_character_event = {
	id = vamp.400
	title = LIFE_ETERNAL
	desc = EVTDESCvamp400
	picture = GFX_evt_vampiress1

	is_triggered_only = yes
	ai = no

	trigger = {
		trait = lahmian_queen
		NOT = { has_character_flag = lahmia0 }
		NOT = { has_landed_title = k_lahmia }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp400
		set_character_flag = lahmia0
	}
}

###############################################################################
# The Lahmian Sisterhood: the seduction
# ROOT is the seductress in her events (persistent targets lahmia_mark and
# lahmia_queen); ROOT is the mark in his, with FROM the seductress.
###############################################################################

# vamp.401 The queen chooses a seductress
# Scope: ROOT is the queen; event_target:lahmia_new_mark the mark. Fired by
# send_a_seductress.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.401
	title = LAHMIA_SEDUCTION
	desc = EVTDESCvamp401
	picture = GFX_evt_lovers

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # A daughter
		name = EVTOPTAvamp401
		trigger = { any_dynasty_member = { is_female = yes vamp_lahmia_free_agent_trigger = yes } }
		save_event_target_as = lahmia_new_queen
		random_dynasty_member = {
			limit = { is_female = yes vamp_lahmia_free_agent_trigger = yes }
			character_event = { id = vamp.402 }
		}
	}

	option = { # An elite agent
		name = EVTOPTBvamp401
		trigger = {
			any_courtier = {
				has_minor_title = lahmian_elite_agent
				vamp_lahmia_free_agent_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		save_event_target_as = lahmia_new_queen
		random_courtier = {
			limit = { has_minor_title = lahmian_elite_agent vamp_lahmia_free_agent_trigger = yes }
			character_event = { id = vamp.402 }
		}
	}

	option = { # Not now
		name = EVTOPTCvamp401
		wealth = 25 # The cost of the decision back
		event_target:lahmia_new_mark = { clr_character_flag = lahmian_target }
	}
}

# vamp.402 The mission
# A daughter gets her orders, or the queen sets out herself.
# Scope: ROOT is the seductress; event_target:lahmia_new_mark the mark,
# event_target:lahmia_new_queen the queen. Fired by vamp.401 and
# seduce_target_personally.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.402
	title = LAHMIA_SEDUCTION
	desc = {
		trigger = { NOT = { character = event_target:lahmia_new_queen } }
		text = EVTDESCvamp402
	}
	desc = { trigger = { character = event_target:lahmia_new_queen } text = EVTDESCvamp402b }
	picture = GFX_evt_vampiress1

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = {
			trigger = { NOT = { character = event_target:lahmia_new_queen } }
			text = EVTOPTAvamp402
		}
		name = { trigger = { character = event_target:lahmia_new_queen } text = EVTOPTAvamp402b }
		add_trait = on_lahmian_mission
		hidden_effect = {
			save_persistent_event_target = {
				name = lahmia_mark
				scope = event_target:lahmia_new_mark
			}
			save_persistent_event_target = {
				name = lahmia_queen
				scope = event_target:lahmia_new_queen
			}
			set_variable = { which = seduction_progress value = 0 }
			event_target:lahmia_new_mark = { set_character_flag = lahmian_target }
			character_event = { id = vamp.403 days = 30 }
		}
	}
}

# vamp.403 At the mark's court
# She picks her approach: the damsel in distress, a lavish ball, or, once his
# wife is dead, comfort for the widower.
# Scope: ROOT is the seductress.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.403
	title = LAHMIA_SEDUCTION
	desc = EVTDESCvamp403
	picture = GFX_evt_vampiress1

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # The damsel in distress
		name = EVTOPTAvamp403
		trigger = {
			vamp_lahmia_mark_alive_trigger = yes
			NOR = { has_character_flag = damselindistress has_character_flag = lahmianbanquet }
		}
		set_character_flag = damselindistress
		event_target:lahmia_mark = { character_event = { id = vamp.405 days = 7 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
	}

	option = { # A lavish ball
		name = EVTOPTBvamp403
		trigger = {
			vamp_lahmia_mark_alive_trigger = yes
			NOT = { has_character_flag = lahmianbanquet }
		}
		set_character_flag = lahmianbanquet
		event_target:lahmia_mark = { character_event = { id = vamp.410 days = 7 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
	}

	option = { # Comfort the widower
		name = EVTOPTCvamp403
		trigger = {
			vamp_lahmia_mark_alive_trigger = yes
			has_character_flag = killed_wife_success
			NOT = { has_character_flag = lahmiancomfort }
		}
		set_character_flag = lahmiancomfort
		event_target:lahmia_mark = { character_event = { id = vamp.420 days = 7 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 200 }
	}

	option = { # Give up
		name = EVTOPTDvamp403
		trigger = { vamp_lahmia_mark_alive_trigger = yes }
		vamp_lahmia_fail_effect = yes
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}

	option = { # The mark is dead
		name = EVTOPTZvamp404
		trigger = { NOT = { vamp_lahmia_mark_alive_trigger = yes } }
		vamp_lahmia_mark_dead_effect = yes
	}
}

		name = EVTOPTAvamp412
		hidden_effect = { FROM = { long_character_event = { id = vamp.413 } } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = gregarious }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = lustful }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = hedonist }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = ambitious }
			modifier = { factor = 2 FROM = { intrigue = 10 } }
			modifier = { factor = 2 FROM = { intrigue = 15 } }
			modifier = { factor = 2 FROM = { intrigue = 20 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = fair } }
			modifier = { factor = 2 FROM = { trait = pretty } }
			modifier = { factor = 3 FROM = { trait = beautiful } }
		}
	}

	option = { # Good night
		name = EVTOPTBvamp412
		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = {
				set_character_flag = lahmia_fail_night
				character_event = { id = vamp.419 days = 1 }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = shy }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = chaste }
			modifier = { factor = 2 is_married = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 8 trait = celibate }
			modifier = { factor = 10 trait = homosexual }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = plain } }
			modifier = { factor = 2 FROM = { trait = ugly } }
			modifier = { factor = 3 FROM = { trait = unsightly } }
		}
	}
}

# vamp.415 The poison
# Does the plot against the mark's wife work? Her intrigue decides: about
# one in three for a clumsy seductress, nearly always for a mistress of
# intrigue. lahmia_poison_feast marks poison at the ball rather than through
# bribed servants.
# Scope: ROOT is the seductress. Fired by vamp.409 and vamp.411.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.415
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { vamp_lahmia_mark_alive_trigger = yes } }
			vamp_lahmia_mark_dead_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			random_list = {
				30 = { event_target:lahmia_mark = { character_event = { id = vamp.416 } } }
				70 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0.43 intrigue = 10 }
					modifier = { factor = 0.43 intrigue = 15 }
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 intrigue = 20 }
					modifier = { factor = 0.1 intrigue = 25 }
					event_target:lahmia_mark = { character_event = { id = vamp.417 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# vamp.430 What to ask for

	}

		trigger = {
			vamp_lahmia_mark_alive_trigger = yes
			NOT = { has_character_flag = return_home_lahmia }
		}
		vamp_lahmia_progress_effect = yes
		event_target:lahmia_mark = { long_character_event = { id = vamp.431 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
	}

	option = { # Troops
		name = EVTOPTBvamp430
		trigger = {
			vamp_lahmia_mark_alive_trigger = yes
			NOT = { has_character_flag = return_home_lahmia }
		}
		vamp_lahmia_progress_effect = yes
		event_target:lahmia_mark = { long_character_event = { id = vamp.435 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
	}

	option = { # Go home
		name = EVTOPTCvamp430
		trigger = {
			vamp_lahmia_mark_alive_trigger = yes
			OR = { ai = no has_character_flag = return_home_lahmia }
		}
		vamp_lahmia_end_mission_effect = yes
		ai_chance = { factor = 1000 }
	}

	option = { # The mark is dead
		name = EVTOPTZvamp404
		trigger = { NOT = { vamp_lahmia_mark_alive_trigger = yes } }
		vamp_lahmia_mark_dead_effect = yes
	}
}

# vamp.431 A plea for gold
# Scope: ROOT is the mark; FROM the seductress.
long_character_event = {
	id = vamp.431
	title = LAHMIA_SEDUCTION
	desc = EVTDESCvamp431
	picture = GFX_evt_cultist

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Pay
		name = EVTOPTAvamp431
		clr_character_flag = lahmia_asked_troops
		if = { limit = { tier = EMPEROR } wealth = -150 }
		else_if = { limit = { tier = KING } wealth = -100 }
		else_if = { limit = { tier = DUKE } wealth = -60 }
		else = { wealth = -30 }
		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.432 days = 1 } }
			character_event = { id = vamp.433 days = 1 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 70
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = lustful }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = charitable }
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				FROM = { check_variable = { which = seduction_progress value = 10 } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				FROM = { check_variable = { which = seduction_progress value = 20 } }
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Refuse
		name = EVTOPTBvamp431
		clr_character_flag = lahmia_asked_troops
		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = {
				set_character_flag = lahmia_fail_gold
				character_event = { id = vamp.419 days = 1 }
			}
			character_event = { id = vamp.434 days = 1 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 20
			modifier = { factor = 3 trait = greedy }
			modifier = { factor = 2 is_married = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 2 NOT = { wealth = 50 } }
		}
	}
}
