		save_event_target_as = combatant_1
		FROM = { save_event_target_as = combatant_2 character_event = { id = HFP.10095 days = 1 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 modifier = { factor = 2 trait = wroth } }
	}

	option = { # Out the window
		name = EVTOPTBvamp442
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
	}
}

###############################################################################
# The Lahmian Sisterhood: new sisters
# A daughter on a recruitment mission looks for a clever young beauty to give
# the blood kiss. She searches every two years until the queen calls her home.
###############################################################################

# vamp.450 A promising mark
# Scope: ROOT is the recruiter. Fired by on_bi_yearly_pulse.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.450
	title = LAHMIA_SEDUCTION
	desc = EVTDESCvamp450
	picture = GFX_evt_cultist

	is_triggered_only = yes
	has_character_flag = recruitment_mission
	prisoner = no

	trigger = {
		trait = on_lahmian_mission
		any_playable_ruler = { any_courtier = { vamp_lahmia_recruit_mark_trigger = yes } }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_playable_ruler = {
			limit = { any_courtier = { vamp_lahmia_recruit_mark_trigger = yes } }
			random_courtier = {
				limit = { vamp_lahmia_recruit_mark_trigger = yes }
				save_event_target_as = lahmia_recruit_mark
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Seek her out
		name = EVTOPTAvamp450
		event_target:lahmia_recruit_mark = { character_event = { id = vamp.451 days = 30 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 80 }
	}

	option = { # Not this one
		name = EVTOPTBvamp450
		ai_chance = { factor = 20 }
	}
}

# vamp.451 The blood kiss is offered
# Scope: ROOT is the young woman; FROM the recruiter.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.451
	desc = EVTDESCvamp451
	picture = GFX_evt_vampiress1

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Accept
		name = EVTOPTAvamp451
		add_trait = disinherited
		prestige = 25
		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.452 } }
			any_sibling = { character_event = { id = vamp.455 days = 1 } }
			father = { character_event = { id = vamp.455 days = 1 } }
			mother = { character_event = { id = vamp.455 days = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { is_married = yes } spouse = { character_event = { id = vamp.456 } } }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = craven }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = lustful }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = hedonist }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = ambitious }
			modifier = { factor = 2 FROM = { intrigue = 15 } }
			modifier = { factor = 2 FROM = { intrigue = 20 } }
		}
	}

	option = { # Refuse
		name = EVTOPTBvamp451
		hidden_effect = { FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.453 } } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = { factor = 3 is_primary_heir = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = proud }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = zealous }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = content }
		}
	}
}

# vamp.452 A new sister
# The young woman takes the blood kiss and joins the recruiter's line.
# Scope: ROOT is the recruiter; FROM the new sister.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.452
	title = LAHMIA_SEDUCTION
	desc = EVTDESCvamp452
	picture = GFX_evt_vampiress1

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp452
		prestige = 25
		if = { limit = { NOT = { intrigue = 25 } } random = { chance = 5 change_intrigue = 1 } }
		FROM = {
			remove_race_trait_effect = yes
			add_trait = creature_human
			add_trait = vampire_lahmian_visible
			set_graphical_culture = lahmian_vampire
			religion = vampiric
			dynasty = ROOT
			vamp_join_bloodline_effect = yes
			set_mother = ROOT
			recalc_succession = yes
			save_event_target_as = lahmia_new_sister
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { character = event_target:lahmia_queen } }
			event_target:lahmia_queen = { character_event = { id = vamp.454 } }
		}
		else = { reverse_banish = FROM }
	}
}

###############################################################################
# The Lahmian Sisterhood: hidden houses
# A lahmian_presence province (establish_lahmian_presence) sends the queen
# money every year, but may draw a witch hunter of its realm. If the queen
# has a Protector at court, the protector meets him in a duel.
###############################################################################

# vamp.470 The hidden houses
# Each hidden house in a land of the Old World faiths sends 10 to 30 gold.
# Each may also be found out by a witch hunter of its realm (vamp.472), at
# most once in five years (vamp_lahmia_suspicion). The queen hears of it
# (vamp.471) until she says not to.
# Scope: ROOT is the queen. Fired by on_yearly_pulse.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.470
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes
	only_rulers = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = lahmian_queen
		any_independent_ruler = {
			any_realm_province = { has_province_modifier = lahmian_presence }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		any_independent_ruler = {
			limit = { any_realm_province = { has_province_modifier = lahmian_presence } }
			any_realm_province = {
				limit = {
					has_province_modifier = lahmian_presence
					religion_group = old_world_group
				}
				ROOT = {
					random_list = {
						33 = { wealth = 10 }
						34 = { wealth = 20 }
						33 = { wealth = 30 }
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_province_modifier = vamp_lahmia_suspicion } }
					random = {
						chance = 10
						add_province_modifier = { name = vamp_lahmia_suspicion duration = 1825 }
						save_event_target_as = lahmia_house
						owner = {
							random_realm_character = {
								limit = {
									is_witch_hunter_trigger = yes
									prisoner = no
									is_incapacitated_trigger = no
									NOT = { trait = on_witch_hunt }
								}
								character_event = { id = vamp.472 }
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = lahmia302 } }
			character_event = { id = vamp.471 }
		}
	}
}

# vamp.472 Strange goings-on
# Scope: ROOT is the witch hunter; FROM the queen; event_target:lahmia_house
# the province.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.472
	desc = EVTDESCvamp472
	picture = GFX_evt_witch_hunters

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Investigate
		name = EVTOPTAvamp472
		prestige = 25
		save_event_target_as = combatant_1
		FROM = {
			save_event_target_as = lahmia_house_queen
			if = {
				limit = { any_courtier = { has_minor_title = lahmian_protector prisoner = no } }
				random_courtier = {
					limit = { has_minor_title = lahmian_protector prisoner = no }
					save_event_target_as = combatant_2
					character_event = { id = HFP.10095 days = 1 }
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = { character_event = { id = vamp.473 days = 60 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 90 modifier = { factor = 2 trait = zealous } }
	}

	option = { # It is nothing
		name = EVTOPTBvamp472
		trigger = { OR = { trait = craven trait = arbitrary trait = content trait = slothful } }
		prestige = -20
		ai_chance = { factor = 10 }
	}
}

###############################################################################
# The dark gift
# A vampire turns a mortal of the realm (decision on the target; the sire is
# event_target:vamp_sire), or a mortal vassal asks for the gift
# (ask_liege_vampirization). Was namespace "vampire" in BR_vampire_system.txt.
###############################################################################

# vamp.500 The dark gift
# The sire comes for a mortal. A ruler may fight; anyone else may try to run.
# Scope: ROOT is the mortal; event_target:vamp_sire the vampire. Fired by the
# turn-a-mortal decision in wh_vampire_decisions.txt.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.500
	title = UNDEATH
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:vamp_sire = {
				NOR = {
					any_friend = { character = ROOT }
					any_lover = { character = ROOT }
					any_spouse = { character = ROOT }
				}
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESCvamp500
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:vamp_sire = {
				any_friend = { character = ROOT }
				NOR = { any_lover = { character = ROOT } any_spouse = { character = ROOT } }
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESCvamp500b
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:vamp_sire = {
				any_lover = { character = ROOT }
				NOT = { any_spouse = { character = ROOT } }
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESCvamp500c
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { event_target:vamp_sire = { any_spouse = { character = ROOT } } }
		text = EVTDESCvamp500d
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_vampire_kiss

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Accept
		name = EVTOPTAvamp500
		opinion = { modifier = opinion_sired_me who = event_target:vamp_sire months = 240000 }
		event_target:vamp_sire = { character_event = { id = vamp.501 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 70
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 NOT = { liege = { character = event_target:vamp_sire } } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 NOT = { trait = content } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 opinion = { who = event_target:vamp_sire value = 20 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 opinion = { who = event_target:vamp_sire value = 50 } }
			modifier = { factor = 2 opinion = { who = event_target:vamp_sire value = 80 } }
			modifier = { factor = 3 trait = ambitious }
			modifier = { factor = 3 any_lover = { character = event_target:vamp_sire } }
		}
	}

	option = { # Fight
		name = EVTOPTBvamp500
		trigger = { is_ruler = yes prisoner = no liege = { character = event_target:vamp_sire } }
		war = { target = event_target:vamp_sire casus_belli = bid_for_independence }
		event_target:vamp_sire = { character_event = { id = vamp.503 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 30
			modifier = { factor = 0 event_target:vamp_sire = { trait = vampire_necrarch_visible } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.85 higher_tier_than = COUNT }
			modifier = { factor = 1.85 higher_tier_than = DUKE }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 NOT = { trait = ambitious } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.75 NOT = { age = 50 } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = trusting }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = paranoid }
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 NOT = { religion = event_target:vamp_sire } }
			modifier = { factor = 2.5 trait = has_nurgles_rot }
			modifier = { factor = 2.5 trait = leper }
			modifier = { factor = 5 trait = immortal }
			modifier = { factor = 0.01 any_lover = { character = event_target:vamp_sire } }
			modifier = { factor = 10 any_rival = { character = event_target:vamp_sire } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 any_friend = { character = event_target:vamp_sire } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 any_spouse = { character = event_target:vamp_sire } }
		}
	}

	option = { # Run
		name = EVTOPTCvamp500
		trigger = { is_ruler = no prisoner = no }
		custom_tooltip = { text = vamp_dark_gift_escape_tt }
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				50 = { # Escapes
					modifier = { factor = 1.9 trait = genius }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = quick }
					modifier = { factor = 0.6 trait = slow }
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = imbecile }
					modifier = { factor = 1.75 trait = strong }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = hardy }
					modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = weak }
					modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = fragile }
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 event_target:vamp_sire = { trait = genius } }
					modifier = { factor = 0.75 event_target:vamp_sire = { trait = quick } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 event_target:vamp_sire = { trait = slow } }
					modifier = { factor = 0.65 event_target:vamp_sire = { trait = strong } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 event_target:vamp_sire = { trait = weak } }
					modifier = { factor = 0.75 event_target:vamp_sire = { intrigue = 15 } }
					banish = yes
					event_target:vamp_sire = { character_event = { id = vamp.502 } }
				}
				50 = { # Caught and turned
					opinion = {
						modifier = opinion_sired_me
						who = event_target:vamp_sire
						months = 240000
					}
					event_target:vamp_sire = { character_event = { id = vamp.501 } }
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 30
			modifier = { factor = 0 event_target:vamp_sire = { trait = vampire_necrarch_visible } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.75 NOT = { trait = ambitious } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.95 NOT = { age = 50 } }
			modifier = { factor = 5 trait = immortal }
			modifier = { factor = 0.01 any_lover = { character = event_target:vamp_sire } }
			modifier = { factor = 10 any_rival = { character = event_target:vamp_sire } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 any_friend = { character = event_target:vamp_sire } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 any_spouse = { character = event_target:vamp_sire } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = trusting }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = paranoid }
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 NOT = { religion = event_target:vamp_sire } }
			modifier = { factor = 2.5 trait = has_nurgles_rot }
			modifier = { factor = 2.5 trait = leper }
		}
	}
}

# vamp.505 A vassal asks for the gift
# Each bloodline has its own tastes: Necrarchs want age and learning, Blood
# Dragons strength, Lahmians women; Strigoi do not share the curse.
# Scope: ROOT is the vampire liege; FROM the vassal. Fired by
# ask_liege_vampirization.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.505
	title = THE_DARK_GIFT
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				NOR = {
					any_friend = { character = ROOT }
					any_lover = { character = ROOT }
					any_spouse = { character = ROOT }
				}
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESCvamp505
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				any_friend = { character = ROOT }
				NOR = { any_lover = { character = ROOT } any_spouse = { character = ROOT } }
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESCvamp505b
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			FROM = { any_lover = { character = ROOT } NOT = { any_spouse = { character = ROOT } } }
		}
		text = EVTDESCvamp505c
	}
	desc = { trigger = { FROM = { any_spouse = { character = ROOT } } } text = EVTDESCvamp505d }
	picture = GFX_evt_vampire_kiss

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Grant it
		name = EVTOPTAvamp505
		root_makes_from_vampire_effect = yes
		FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.506 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 40
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				trait = vampire_necrarch_visible
				FROM = { OR = { NOT = { age = 30 } NOT = { learning = 12 } } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				trait = vampire_blood_visible
				FROM = {
					OR = {
						trait = weak
						trait = fragile
						trait = soft
						trait = maimed
						trait = incapable
						trait = blinded
						trait = eunuch
						trait = is_malnourished
						trait = is_fat
						trait = addict
						NOT = { martial = 10 }
						NOT = { age = 16 }
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = { factor = 0 trait = vampire_strigoi_visible }
			modifier = { factor = 0 trait = vampire_lahmian_visible FROM = { is_female = no } }
			modifier = { factor = 3 FROM = { any_lover = { character = ROOT } } }
		}
	}

	option = { # Not worthy
		name = EVTOPTBvamp505
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				ai = no
				FROM = { NOT = { martial = 12 } }
				FROM = { NOT = { intrigue = 12 } }
				FROM = { NOT = { stewardship = 12 } }
				FROM = { NOT = { diplomacy = 12 } }
			}
		}
		FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.507 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 60 }
	}
}

###############################################################################
# The coven
# A sire takes a fledgling into the dynasty (makeavampireheir), or a
# fledgling asks to be taken in (askvampliegedynasty).
###############################################################################

# vamp.510 Welcomed into the bloodline
# Scope: ROOT is the fledgling; event_target:vamp_sire the sire. Fired by
# makeavampireheir.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.510
	title = A_GREAT_HONOR
	desc = EVTDESCvamp510
	picture = GFX_evt_blood_calice

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Accept
		name = EVTOPTAvamp510
		prestige = 200
		vamp_join_sire_dynasty_effect = yes
		event_target:vamp_sire = { character_event = { id = vamp.511 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 60
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 is_ruler = yes lower_tier_than = event_target:vamp_sire }
			modifier = { factor = 1.75 is_ruler = no }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 any_lover = { character = event_target:vamp_sire } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 any_spouse = { character = event_target:vamp_sire } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 opinion = { who = event_target:vamp_sire value = 20 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 opinion = { who = event_target:vamp_sire value = 50 } }
			modifier = { factor = 2 opinion = { who = event_target:vamp_sire value = 80 } }
		}
	}

	option = { # Refuse
		name = EVTOPTBvamp510
		event_target:vamp_sire = { character_event = { id = vamp.512 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 40
			modifier = { factor = 1.75 is_ruler = yes higher_tier_than = event_target:vamp_sire }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 is_ruler = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 prestige = 1000 }
			modifier = { factor = 2 any_rival = { character = event_target:vamp_sire } }
		}
	}
}

# vamp.512 The fledgling refuses
# Scope: ROOT is the sire; FROM the fledgling.

	id = vamp.512
	title = A_GREAT_HONOR
	desc = EVTDESCvamp512
	picture = GFX_evt_blood_calice

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp512
		prestige = -100
		opinion = { modifier = opinion_v_rejected_adoption who = FROM months = 360 }
		reverse_opinion = { modifier = opinion_v_rejected_adoption who = FROM months = 360 }
	}
}

# vamp.513 A fledgling asks to join the coven
# Scope: ROOT is the sire; event_target:vamp_fledgling the fledgling. Fired by
# askvampliegedynasty.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.513
	title = A_GREAT_HONOR
	desc = EVTDESCvamp513
	picture = GFX_evt_blood_calice

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Accept
		name = EVTOPTAvamp513
		event_target:vamp_fledgling = { character_event = { id = vamp.514 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 60
			modifier = { factor = 1.75 is_ruler = no }
			modifier = { factor = 1.75 NOT = { num_of_children = 5 } }
			modifier = { factor = 2 NOT = { num_of_children = 1 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 any_lover = { character = event_target:vamp_fledgling } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 any_spouse = { character = event_target:vamp_fledgling } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 opinion = { who = event_target:vamp_fledgling value = 20 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 opinion = { who = event_target:vamp_fledgling value = 50 } }
			modifier = { factor = 2 opinion = { who = event_target:vamp_fledgling value = 80 } }
		}
	}

	option = { # Not yet
		name = EVTOPTBvamp513
		event_target:vamp_fledgling = { character_event = { id = vamp.515 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 40
			modifier = { factor = 1.75 num_of_children = 2 }
			modifier = { factor = 1.75 num_of_children = 5 }
			modifier = { factor = 2 num_of_children = 10 }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = paranoid }
			modifier = { factor = 2 any_rival = { character = event_target:vamp_fledgling } }
		}
	}

	option = { # Never
		name = EVTOPTCvamp513
		trigger = { ai = no }
		event_target:vamp_fledgling = { character_event = { id = vamp.516 } }
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Sire and get
# Over the centuries a vampire may come to hate the sire who made them; a
# Mahtmasi may come to love theirs, and a Mahtmasi's love ends in hunger.
# All three are rare: five-year random lists vamp_sire_rivalry,
# vamp_mahtmasi_love and vamp_mahtmasi_hunger (were MTTH 250, 250 and 100
# years).
###############################################################################

# vamp.520 Under the sire's thumb
# Rank, prestige, ambition and outshining the sire make it likelier; liking
# the sire makes it rarer.
# Scope: ROOT is the vampire. Fired by on_five_year_pulse (vamp_sire_rivalry).
character_event = {
	id = vamp.520
	desc = { trigger = { father = { vamp_resented_sire_trigger = yes } } text = EVTDESCvamp520 }
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = { father = { vamp_resented_sire_trigger = yes } }
			mother = { vamp_resented_sire_trigger = yes }
		}
		text = EVTDESCvamp520b
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_vampire_kiss

	is_triggered_only = yes
	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			trait = vampire_carstein_visible
			trait = vampire_blood_visible
			trait = vampire_strigoi_visible
			trait = vampire_necrarch_visible
			trait = vampire_jade_visible
		}
		OR = {
			father = { vamp_resented_sire_trigger = yes }
			mother = { vamp_resented_sire_trigger = yes }
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 primary_title = { higher_real_tier_than = BARON } }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 primary_title = { higher_real_tier_than = COUNT } }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 primary_title = { higher_real_tier_than = DUKE } }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 prestige = 500 }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 prestige = 1000 }
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = ambitious }
		modifier = { factor = 1.33 trait = vampire_carstein_visible }
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			OR = {
				father = {
					vamp_resented_sire_trigger = yes
					NOT = { martial = ROOT }
					NOT = { intrigue = ROOT }
				}
				mother = {
					vamp_resented_sire_trigger = yes
					NOT = { martial = ROOT }
					NOT = { intrigue = ROOT }
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			OR = {
				AND = {
					father = { vamp_resented_sire_trigger = yes }
					NOT = { opinion = { who = father value = -25 } }
				}
				AND = {
					mother = { vamp_resented_sire_trigger = yes }
					NOT = { opinion = { who = mother value = -25 } }
				}
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			OR = {
				AND = {
					father = { vamp_resented_sire_trigger = yes }
					opinion = { who = father value = 50 }
				}
				AND = {
					mother = { vamp_resented_sire_trigger = yes }
					opinion = { who = mother value = 50 }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp520
		if = { limit = { father = { vamp_resented_sire_trigger = yes } } add_rival = father }
		else = { add_rival = mother }
	}
}

# vamp.523 Love for the sire
# Scope: ROOT is the Mahtmasi. Fired by on_five_year_pulse (vamp_mahtmasi_love).
character_event = {
	id = vamp.523
	desc = EVTDESCvamp523
	picture = GFX_evt_mahtmasi_vampire

	is_triggered_only = yes
	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no

	trigger = {
		trait = vampire_mahtmasi_visible
		OR = {
			mother = { vamp_mahtmasi_sire_trigger = yes }
			father = { vamp_mahtmasi_sire_trigger = yes }
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 primary_title = { higher_real_tier_than = COUNT } }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 prestige = 500 }
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = lustful }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = chaste }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp523
		if = {
			limit = { mother = { vamp_mahtmasi_sire_trigger = yes } }
			mother = { add_lover = ROOT character_event = { id = vamp.524 days = 5 random = 5 } }
		}
		else = {
			father = { add_lover = ROOT character_event = { id = vamp.524 days = 5 random = 5 } }
		}
	}
}

# vamp.525 The hunger of love
# A Mahtmasi can no longer bear to be apart from a lover, and seeks to devour
# them. Sooner for a Mahtmasi lover, sooner still for one's own get.
# Scope: ROOT is the Mahtmasi. Fired by on_five_year_pulse
# (vamp_mahtmasi_hunger).
character_event = {
	id = vamp.525
	desc = EVTDESCvamp525
	picture = GFX_evt_mahtmasi_vampire

	is_triggered_only = yes
	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no

	trigger = {
		trait = vampire_mahtmasi_visible
		any_lover = { prisoner = no }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 any_lover = { trait = vampire_mahtmasi_visible } }
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			any_lover = {
				trait = vampire_mahtmasi_visible
				OR = { mother = { character = ROOT } father = { character = ROOT } }
			}
		}
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 primary_title = { higher_real_tier_than = DUKE } }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 prestige = 500 }
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = lustful }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp525
		random_lover = {
			limit = { prisoner = no }
			preferred_limit = { trait = vampire_mahtmasi_visible }
			character_event = { id = vamp.526 }
		}
	}
}

# vamp.526 Devoured by love
# Scope: ROOT is the lover; FROM the hungry Mahtmasi.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.526
	desc = EVTDESCvamp526
	picture = GFX_evt_mahtmasi_vampire

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Let it happen
		name = EVTOPTAvamp526
		custom_tooltip = { text = vamp_mahtmasi_consumed_tt }
		FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.527 } }
		hidden_effect = {
			if = { limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes } antideath_effect = yes }
			else = { death = { death_reason = death_murder killer = FROM } }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = vampire_mahtmasi_visible }
			modifier = { factor = 0.2 trait = craven }
		}
	}

	option = { # Cast them out
		name = EVTOPTBvamp526
		prestige = -200
		random_list = {
			50 = { }
			25 = { add_trait = stressed }
			25 = { add_trait = depressed }
		}
		remove_lover = FROM
		add_rival = FROM
		FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.528 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
	}
}

###############################################################################
# The Vampire Wars
###############################################################################

# vamp.600 The Vampire Wars begin
# On startup, the multiplayer host sends vamp.601 to the holder of
# e_sylvania, once per game.
# Scope: ROOT is the host player.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.600
	hide_window = yes

	ai = no
	only_playable = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_startup

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_global_flag = vampire_wars_started }
		is_multiplayer_host_character = yes
	}
	immediate = {
		set_global_flag = vampire_wars_started
		e_sylvania = { holder_scope = { character_event = { id = vamp.601 } } }
	}
}

# vamp.601 The dead march
# Vlad's great spell is done. He sends his host against Ostermark (the AI
# always does), or a player may strike elsewhere with a smaller host. Every
# other player hears of it (vamp.602). The game rule warhammer_vampire_wars
# sets the size of the host, or turns the war off.
# Scope: ROOT is the holder of e_sylvania with the Carstein Ring.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.601
	title = CONQUEST
	desc = EVTDESCvamp601
	picture = "GFX_evt_zombies"
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	religion = vampiric

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_artifact = carstein_ring
		primary_title = { higher_tier_than = KING }
		NOT = { has_global_flag = vamp_vlad_war_begun }
		NOT = { has_game_rule = { name = warhammer_vampire_wars value = off } }
	}
	immediate = {
		set_global_flag = vamp_vlad_war_begun
		k_ostermark = { save_event_target_as = vamp_vlad_goal }
		366 = { save_event_target_as = vamp_vlad_goal_seat } # c_bechafen
		any_player = {
			limit = { NOT = { character = ROOT } }
			if = {
				limit = { vamp_old_world_culture_trigger = yes }
				character_event = { id = vamp.602 days = 7 }
			}
			else = { character_event = { id = vamp.602 days = 30 } }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp601
		#To war: Ostermark
		primary_title = { set_title_flag = vampire_wars_instigator }
		vamp_vlad_host_effect = yes
		vamp_vlad_march_effect = yes
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBvamp601
		#I will strike elsewhere
		trigger = { ai = no }
		primary_title = { set_title_flag = vampire_wars_instigator }
		set_character_flag = vamp_vlad_own_path
		vamp_vlad_small_host_effect = yes
	}
}

# vamp.602 The dead march
# Players hear that Vlad von Carstein's host has risen. Old World realms hear
# within a week, distant ones a month later.
# Scope: ROOT is a player; FROM is Vlad.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.602
	title = RISE_OF_THE_UNDEAD
	desc = { text = EVTDESCvamp602 trigger = { vamp_old_world_culture_trigger = yes } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCvamp602b trigger = { vamp_old_world_culture_trigger = no } }
	picture = "GFX_evt_carstein"

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp602
		trigger = { vamp_old_world_culture_trigger = yes }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBvamp602
		trigger = { vamp_old_world_culture_trigger = no }
	}
}

# vamp.605 The march of conquest
# Each year at peace, Vlad looks for the next province of the Empire on his
# road to Altdorf: Talabecland, Hochland, Middenland, Ostland, Nordland,
# Westerland and last the Reikland. Each step is offered once (vamp.606),
# after he holds Sylvania and Ostermark. A kingdom counts as his when its
# holder or its capital county is in his realm.
# Scope: ROOT is Vlad von Carstein.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.605
	hide_window = yes

	religion = vampiric
	only_independent = yes
	has_character_flag = vlad_von_carstein

	is_triggered_only = yes # yearly pulse

	trigger = {
		war = no
		has_artifact = carstein_ring
		primary_title = { higher_tier_than = KING }
		NOT = { has_character_flag = vamp_vlad_own_path }
		NOT = { has_game_rule = { name = warhammer_vampire_wars value = off } }
		OR = {
			k_sylvania = { holder_scope = { same_realm = ROOT } }
			386 = { owner = { same_realm = ROOT } } # c_drakenhof
		}
		OR = {
			k_ostermark = { holder_scope = { same_realm = ROOT } }
			366 = { owner = { same_realm = ROOT } } # c_bechafen
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		vamp_vlad_next_goal_effect = yes
	}
}

# vamp.606 The march of conquest
# Vlad sends his host against the next kingdom of the Empire. The AI raises
# fresh dead for it; a player may give up the road and choose their own
# wars.
# Scope: ROOT is Vlad; event_target:vamp_vlad_goal the kingdom,
# event_target:vamp_vlad_goal_seat its capital province.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.606
	title = CONQUEST
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCvamp606
		trigger = { event_target:vamp_vlad_goal = { title = k_talabecland } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCvamp606b
		trigger = { event_target:vamp_vlad_goal = { title = k_hochland } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCvamp606c
		trigger = { event_target:vamp_vlad_goal = { title = k_middenland } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCvamp606d
		trigger = { event_target:vamp_vlad_goal = { title = k_ostland } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCvamp606e
		trigger = { event_target:vamp_vlad_goal = { title = k_nordland } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCvamp606f
		trigger = { event_target:vamp_vlad_goal = { title = k_westerland } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCvamp606g
		trigger = { event_target:vamp_vlad_goal = { title = k_reikland } }
	}
	picture = "GFX_evt_carstein"
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp606
		#Victory is inevitable
		if = { limit = { ai = yes } vamp_vlad_reinforce_effect = yes }
		vamp_vlad_march_effect = yes
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBvamp606
		#I choose my own road
		trigger = { ai = no }
		set_character_flag = vamp_vlad_own_path
	}
}

# vamp.609 Vlad falls
# When Vlad von Carstein dies for good, the dead he raised fall back into
# their graves and the Vampire Wars end. Players hear of it (vamp.610).
# Scope: ROOT is the dead Vlad.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.609
	hide_window = yes

	has_character_flag = vlad_von_carstein

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_death

	immediate = {
		set_global_flag = undead_disbanded
		set_global_flag = vampire_wars_concluded
		disband_event_forces = vlad_undead
		any_player = {
			if = {
				limit = { vamp_old_world_culture_trigger = yes }
				character_event = { id = vamp.610 days = 7 }
			}
			else = { character_event = { id = vamp.610 days = 30 } }
		}
	}
}

# vamp.610 The dead lie still
# Vlad's host returns to the earth. His kin and the vampire faithful mourn the
# loss; everyone else breathes again.
# Scope: ROOT is a player; FROM is the dead Vlad.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.610
	title = DISASTER
	desc = { text = EVTDESCvamp610 trigger = { NOR = { dynasty = FROM religion = vampiric } } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCvamp610b trigger = { OR = { dynasty = FROM religion = vampiric } } }
	picture = "GFX_evt_zombies"

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp610
		trigger = { NOR = { dynasty = FROM religion = vampiric } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBvamp610
		trigger = { OR = { dynasty = FROM religion = vampiric } }
	}
}

# vamp.615 Risen again
# The Carstein Ring drags its wearer back from a death blow (from
# antideath_effect). They walk free of any prison and pay in prestige; the
# world learns the ring's secret. If vampires of their realm already reach
# for their crown, they may crush the upstarts' claims.
# Scope: ROOT is the ring holder.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.615
	title = EVTTITLEvamp615
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCvamp615
		trigger = { NOT = { any_realm_character = { vamp_crown_claimant_trigger = yes } } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCvamp615b
		trigger = { any_realm_character = { vamp_crown_claimant_trigger = yes } }
	}
	picture = "GFX_evt_carstein"

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = { limit = { prisoner = yes } imprison = no }
		set_global_flag = carstein_ring_revealed
		change_variable = { which = carstein_death_count value = 1 }
		primary_title = { save_event_target_as = vamp_vlad_crown }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp615
		prestige = -100
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBvamp615
		#I will not be replaced
		trigger = { any_realm_character = { vamp_crown_claimant_trigger = yes } }
		prestige = -50
		any_realm_character = {
			limit = { vamp_crown_claimant_trigger = yes }
			remove_claim = event_target:vamp_vlad_crown
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_humiliated_me who = ROOT years = 10 }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
}

# vamp.620 The refugees of the Empire
# Soldiers driven from the lands Vlad took flock to the lord who fought him
# (from the vlad_subjugation CB). They join as regiments from the shared
# refugee pool; the first muster is larger and may bring the elite troops of
# the lost kingdom. Nearby rulers of the same faith get a share (vamp.621).
# Repeats every 70 days while refugees remain.
# Scope: ROOT is the defender or an independent ruler of their faith;
# event_target:vamp_invader is Vlad, event_target:target_title the lost
# kingdom.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.620
	title = RAVAGES_OF_UNDEATH
	desc = EVTDESCvamp620
	picture = "GFX_evt_carstein"

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		check_variable = { which = global_imperial_refugees value = 1 }
	}
	immediate = {
		any_independent_ruler = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { character = ROOT }
				NOT = { has_character_flag = fought_in_vampire_wars }
				religion_group = ROOT
				NOT = { religion_group = necromantic_group }
				OR = {
					distance = { where = ROOT distance < 1000 }
					AND = { culture_group = ROOT distance = { where = ROOT distance < 2000 } }
				}
			}
			if = { limit = { ai = no } character_event = { id = vamp.621 days = 20 } }
			else = { character_event = { id = vamp.621 days = 40 random = 8 } }
		}
		any_playable_ruler = {
			limit = { independent = no tier = KING culture_group = ROOT religion_group = ROOT }
			character_event = { id = vamp.621 days = 68 random = 8 }
		}
		repeat_event = { id = vamp.620 days = 70 }
	}
	fail_trigger_effect = { clr_character_flag = vamp_refugees_received }

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp620
		#First muster
		trigger = { NOT = { has_character_flag = vamp_refugees_received } }
		set_character_flag = vamp_refugees_received
		vamp_refugee_regiment_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = global_imperial_refugees value = 1 } }
			vamp_refugee_regiment_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = global_imperial_refugees value = 1 } }
			vamp_refugee_missile_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = global_imperial_refugees value = 3 } }
			vamp_refugee_regiment_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = global_imperial_refugees value = 3 } }
			vamp_refugee_elite_effect = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp620
		#Later musters cost two refugees per extra regiment
		trigger = { has_character_flag = vamp_refugees_received }
		vamp_refugee_regiment_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = global_imperial_refugees value = 2 } }
			vamp_refugee_regiment_effect = yes
			subtract_variable = { which = global_imperial_refugees value = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = global_imperial_refugees value = 2 } }
			vamp_refugee_missile_effect = yes
			subtract_variable = { which = global_imperial_refugees value = 1 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBvamp620
		#Send them on to others
		piety = 25
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

# vamp.621 Refugees at the gate
# A share of the Empire's refugees reaches a ruler who fought beside the
# defender, or a nearby ruler of their faith.
# Scope: ROOT is the receiving ruler; event_target:target_title the lost
# kingdom.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.621
	title = RAVAGES_OF_UNDEATH
	desc = EVTDESCvamp620
	picture = "GFX_evt_carstein"

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		check_variable = { which = global_imperial_refugees value = 1 }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp620
		clr_character_flag = fought_in_vampire_wars
		vamp_refugee_regiment_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = global_imperial_refugees value = 1 } }
			vamp_refugee_missile_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = global_imperial_refugees value = 3 } }
			vamp_refugee_elite_effect = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBvamp620
		clr_character_flag = fought_in_vampire_wars
		piety = 25
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

# vamp.630 Serve in life, or in death
# When a vlad_subjugation war starts, the defender gets Vlad's offer: fight,
# surrender (they and their great vassals become his vassals), or kneel
# willingly and take his faith as well. The AI weighs its own nerve and how
# many Elector Counts already serve Vlad. Vlad hears the answer (vamp.631).
# Scope: ROOT is the defender; FROM the attacker.
letter_event = {
	id = vamp.630
	desc = EVTDESCvamp630
	picture = "GFX_evt_carstein"
	border = GFX_event_letter_frame_war

	only_rulers = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes # vlad_subjugation CB on_add

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp630
		#Never
		prestige = 200
		FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.631 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				OR = { trait = zealous trait = ambitious trait = wroth trait = brave }
			}
			modifier = { factor = 2 primary_title = { higher_tier_than = DUKE } }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBvamp630
		#Surrender
		prestige = -100
		vamp_vlad_submit_effect = yes
		FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.631 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = { trait = zealous primary_title = { higher_tier_than = KING } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				OR = {
					trait = patient
					trait = humble
					trait = diligent
					trait = arbitrary
					trait = greedy
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				FROM = {
					num_of_government_vassals = { government = elector_count_government value = 1 }
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				FROM = {
					num_of_government_vassals = { government = elector_count_government value = 3 }
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				FROM = {
					num_of_government_vassals = { government = elector_count_government value = 6 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCvamp630
		#Kneel and take the dark faith
		prestige = -200
		vamp_vlad_submit_effect = yes
		FROM = { reverse_religion = ROOT }
		FROM = { character_event = { id = vamp.631 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 5
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = { trait = zealous primary_title = { higher_tier_than = KING } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				OR = {
					trait = craven
					trait = slothful
					trait = content
					trait = slow
					trait = imbecile
					trait = inbred
					trait = weak
					trait = cynical
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				FROM = {
					num_of_government_vassals = { government = elector_count_government value = 1 }
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				FROM = {
					num_of_government_vassals = { government = elector_count_government value = 3 }
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				FROM = {
					num_of_government_vassals = { government = elector_count_government value = 6 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# vamp.631 The answer
# Vlad hears whether the defender refused, surrendered, or knelt and took his
# faith (vamp.630).
# Scope: ROOT is Vlad; FROM the defender.
character_event = {
	id = vamp.631
	desc = { text = EVTDESCvamp631 trigger = { FROM = { NOT = { same_realm = ROOT } } } }
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCvamp631b
		trigger = { FROM = { same_realm = ROOT NOT = { religion = ROOT } } }
	}
	desc = { text = EVTDESCvamp631c trigger = { FROM = { same_realm = ROOT religion = ROOT } } }
	picture = "GFX_evt_carstein"

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAvamp631
		trigger = { FROM = { NOT = { same_realm = ROOT } } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBvamp631
		trigger = { FROM = { same_realm = ROOT } }
	}
}
